' Excel Formula Atlas - single-module VBA distribution
' Turn an unfamiliar workbook into an interactive map of formula references.
' This text file contains the VBA engine, HTML viewer, and local navigation helper.
'
' QUICK START (Windows desktop Excel)
' 1. Open this file in a text editor. Select all and copy the entire contents.
' 2. In Excel, press Alt+F11 and choose Insert > Module to create a NEW module.
' 3. In that NEW module's code window only, press Ctrl+A and paste the entire TXT.
'    This replaces any automatic declaration line. Keep existing user modules.
'    Use a standard module, not ThisWorkbook or a worksheet code window.
' 4. Return to Excel. Press Alt+F8, choose ExcelMap_Start, and click Run.
' 5. Choose 1 for the current workbook or 2 for a file, then an output folder.
' 6. Explore the map. Select a referenced cell and use Open in Excel to jump back.
'
' EXPLORING THE MAP
' Left drag selects an area and highlights the cells/ranges that feed it.
' Right drag pans the map. The mouse wheel zooms around the pointer.
' Click a cell to inspect its actual nearby table, value, and formula.
' Click a connection to highlight its endpoints and related formula flow.
' Search worksheet names, cell addresses, nearby labels, and formulas.
' The viewer starts in English; its language button also offers Korean.
' Arrows point from source values to the formulas that use them.
' Dashed membership links identify formula cells inside referenced ranges.
' Excel shows analysis stages in its status bar. The HTML shows preparation
' stages, actual completed-item progress, and elapsed time while rendering.
' Progress counts completed work; it is not an estimate of time remaining.
'
' LOCAL FILES AND EXCEL NAVIGATION
' No add-in, installer, Python, or extra source file is required for normal use.
' The macro writes one HTML file to your chosen folder. It extracts its embedded
' PowerShell navigation helper and connection files into a temporary folder.
' Workbook contents are processed locally. The helper listens only on this PC
' at 127.0.0.1 and uses a temporary session token. It does not upload your workbook.
' The exported HTML contains workbook data. Share it only with intended readers.
' Excel navigation requires Windows PowerShell and an open source workbook.
' Workplace macro or process policies may prevent the helper from starting.
' This code does not change those policies. The standalone HTML remains usable.
' A newly selected file opens read-only, with external-link updates disabled and
' VBA macros disabled. Already-open workbooks are reused. Excel may separately
' prompt about legacy Excel 4.0 macros. This tool does not edit source cell values
' or formulas, convert the source file, or automatically save the source workbook.
'
' STOPPING AND SAVING
' Run ExcelMap_Stop to stop the navigation connection. The HTML file is retained.
' Stop before closing the workbook that holds this code or using Save As on it.
' The included Auto_Close is a convenience for manual closing. VBA Workbook.Close
' does not run Auto_Close. If an Auto_Close already exists, merge the call to
' FM_StopNavigation into it instead of adding a second Auto_Close procedure.
' Save the code-hosting workbook as .xlsm if you want to keep the macro for later.
' The navigation session expires after two hours. Hidden sheets are not unhidden.
' Regenerate the map after source values, formulas, workbook name, or path change.
'
' SCOPE AND LIMITS
' This is static reference analysis, not a calculation engine or workbook audit.
' Conditional formulas include possible branches; dynamic and external references
' may require review. Read the notices in the map before relying on coverage.
' Formula scan cap: 50,000. Captured geometry: up to 2,000 rows and 256 columns.
' Context capture starts with 100 rows / 40 columns and referenced-cell neighbors,
' capped at 20,000 positions per worksheet. Uncaptured cells are identified.
' Visible connections: up to 200 in selected flow, or 700 in the overview.
' The HTML is a snapshot. Charts, pictures, and every Excel style are not replicated.
' See the repository documentation for supported references and verification scope.
' ============================================================================

Option Explicit

Private Const FM_MAX_FORMULAS As Long = 50000
Private Const FM_MAX_NATIVE_NODES As Long = 80
Private Const FM_MAX_MEMBERSHIP_CHECKS As Long = 2000000
Private mFMNodes As Collection, mFMEdges As Collection, mFMIssues As Collection
Private mFMNodeIndex As Object, mFMEdgeIndex As Object, mFMSheets As Collection
Private mFMSource As Workbook
Private mFMFormulaCount As Long, mFMTotalFormulas As Double, mFMLimited As Boolean
Private mFMWorksheetJSON As String
Private mFMProgressBusy As Boolean
Private mFMProgressLastTick As Single
Private mFMProgressStage As Long, mFMProgressSheetDone As Long

Public Sub ExcelMap_Start()
    Dim currentBook As Workbook, src As Workbook
    Dim currentBookName As String, currentLabel As String
    Dim answer As Variant, sourceChoice As Long
    Dim selectedPath As String, outputFolder As String, htmlPath As String
    Dim failureText As String
    If mFMProgressBusy Then
        MsgBox "A formula map is already being created. Please wait for it to finish.", vbInformation
        Exit Sub
    End If
    On Error GoTo Failed

    Set currentBook = Application.ActiveWorkbook
    If Not currentBook Is Nothing Then
        currentBookName = currentBook.Name
        currentLabel = currentBook.Name
        If Len(currentLabel) > 60 Then currentLabel = Left$(currentLabel, 57) & "..."
    Else
        currentLabel = "No active workbook"
    End If

    answer = Application.InputBox( _
        Prompt:="Choose the source workbook." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
            "1. Current workbook: " & currentLabel & vbCrLf & _
            "2. Choose an Excel file" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
            "Enter 1 or 2, or Cancel.", _
        Title:="Excel formula map", Default:=1, Type:=1)
    If VarType(answer) = vbBoolean Then Exit Sub
    If answer <> Fix(answer) Or answer < 1 Or answer > 2 Then
        MsgBox "Enter 1 or 2.", vbInformation
        Exit Sub
    End If
    sourceChoice = CLng(answer)

    If sourceChoice = 1 Then
        If currentBook Is Nothing Then
            MsgBox "Open a workbook first, or run again and choose option 2.", vbInformation
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Else
        With Application.FileDialog(3)
            .Title = "Choose the Excel workbook to analyze"
            .AllowMultiSelect = False
            .Filters.Clear
            .Filters.Add "Excel workbooks", "*.xlsx;*.xlsm;*.xlsb;*.xls"
            If .Show <> -1 Then Exit Sub
            If .SelectedItems.Count = 0 Then Exit Sub
            selectedPath = CStr(.SelectedItems(1))
        End With
    End If

    With Application.FileDialog(4)
        .Title = "Choose the folder for the HTML map"
        .AllowMultiSelect = False
        If .Show <> -1 Then Exit Sub
        If .SelectedItems.Count = 0 Then Exit Sub
        outputFolder = CStr(.SelectedItems(1))
    End With

    ' Resolve only after both dialogs finish so cancelling does not open a file.
    Set src = FM_ResolveSource(sourceChoice, currentBookName, selectedPath)
    If sourceChoice = 1 Then
        If Not src Is currentBook Then Err.Raise 5, "ExcelMap_Start", _
            "The original active workbook changed; run again."
    End If
    FM_StopNavigation
    htmlPath = FM_BuildFile(src.Name, outputFolder, True, False)
    On Error GoTo NavigationUnavailable
    StartFormulaMapNavigation htmlPath, True
    Exit Sub

NavigationUnavailable:
    failureText = Err.Description
    MsgBox "The HTML map was created, but the Excel navigation connection could not start." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        failureText & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        "HTML file:" & vbCrLf & htmlPath & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        "Open the HTML file directly to explore the map.", vbExclamation
    Exit Sub

Failed:
    failureText = Err.Description
    MsgBox "The formula map could not be created." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        failureText, vbExclamation
End Sub

Public Function FM_ResolveSource(ByVal choice As Long, ByVal currentBookName As String, Optional ByVal filePath As String = "") As Workbook
    Dim book As Workbook, source As Workbook, fso As Object
    Dim fullPath As String, extension As String
    If choice = 1 Then
        If Len(currentBookName) = 0 Then Err.Raise 5, "FM_ResolveSource", _
            "No active workbook was captured."
        On Error Resume Next
        Set source = Application.Workbooks(currentBookName)
        On Error GoTo 0
        If source Is Nothing Then Err.Raise 5, "FM_ResolveSource", _
            "The original active workbook is no longer open."
        Set FM_ResolveSource = source
        Exit Function
    End If
    If choice <> 2 Then Err.Raise 5, "FM_ResolveSource", _
        "Source choice must be 1 or 2."
    If Len(filePath) = 0 Then Err.Raise 5, "FM_ResolveSource", _
        "No source file path was supplied."

    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    fullPath = fso.GetAbsolutePathName(filePath)
    extension = LCase$(CStr(fso.GetExtensionName(fullPath)))
    Select Case extension
        Case "xlsx", "xlsm", "xlsb", "xls"
        Case Else
            Err.Raise 5, "FM_ResolveSource", _
                "Choose an Excel workbook (.xlsx, .xlsm, .xlsb or .xls)."
    End Select

    For Each book In Application.Workbooks
        If StrComp(book.FullName, fullPath, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            Set FM_ResolveSource = book
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next book
    If Not fso.FileExists(fullPath) Then Err.Raise 53, "FM_ResolveSource", _
        "The selected file was not found: " & fullPath
    Set FM_ResolveSource = FM_OpenSourceReadOnly(fullPath)
End Function

Private Function FM_OpenSourceReadOnly(ByVal fullPath As String) As Workbook
    Dim oldSecurity As Long, oldEvents As Boolean, openedBook As Workbook
    Dim errorNumber As Long, errorText As String, errorSource As String
    oldSecurity = Application.AutomationSecurity
    oldEvents = Application.EnableEvents
    On Error GoTo OpenFailed
    Application.AutomationSecurity = 3
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Set openedBook = Application.Workbooks.Open( _
        Filename:=fullPath, UpdateLinks:=0, ReadOnly:=True, _
        IgnoreReadOnlyRecommended:=True, AddToMru:=False)

RestoreApplication:
    On Error Resume Next
    Application.AutomationSecurity = oldSecurity
    Application.EnableEvents = oldEvents
    On Error GoTo 0
    If errorNumber <> 0 Then Err.Raise errorNumber, errorSource, errorText
    If openedBook Is Nothing Then Err.Raise 5, "FM_OpenSourceReadOnly", _
        "The selected workbook could not be opened."
    Set FM_OpenSourceReadOnly = openedBook
    Exit Function

OpenFailed:
    errorNumber = Err.Number
    errorText = Err.Description
    errorSource = Err.Source
    Resume RestoreApplication
End Function

Public Sub ExcelMap_Stop()
    FM_StopNavigation
    MsgBox "The Excel navigation connection has stopped. The HTML map is retained.", vbInformation
End Sub

Public Sub Auto_Close()
    FM_StopNavigation
End Sub

Public Function FM_BuildFile(ByVal sourceBookName As String, ByVal outputFolder As String, Optional ByVal silent As Boolean = True, Optional ByVal createExcelReport As Boolean = True) As String
    Dim oldScreen As Boolean, oldEvents As Boolean, oldAlerts As Boolean, oldStatus As Variant
    Dim oldCancel As Long, ws As Worksheet, formulas As Collection, cell As Range
    Dim refs As Collection, problems As Collection, ref As Object, item As Variant
    Dim target As String, source As String, report As Workbook, base As String, htmlPath As String
    Dim errorNumber As Long, errorText As String
    Dim fmProgressSheet As Long, oldDisplayStatusBar As Boolean
    Dim fmProgressData As String, fmProgressHTML As String
    If mFMProgressBusy Then Err.Raise 5, "FormulaMap", _
        "A formula map is already being created."

    oldScreen = Application.ScreenUpdating: oldEvents = Application.EnableEvents
    oldAlerts = Application.DisplayAlerts: oldStatus = Application.StatusBar
    oldCancel = Application.EnableCancelKey
    oldDisplayStatusBar = Application.DisplayStatusBar
    On Error GoTo Failed
    mFMProgressBusy = True: mFMProgressLastTick = -1
    mFMProgressStage = 1: mFMProgressSheetDone = 0
    Application.DisplayStatusBar = True
    Set mFMSource = Application.Workbooks(sourceBookName)
    If Len(Dir$(outputFolder, vbDirectory)) = 0 Then Err.Raise 5, , "Output folder was not found: " & outputFolder
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False: Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False: Application.EnableCancelKey = xlErrorHandler
    FM_ShowProgress "1/5 Finding formulas", 0, 0, "Preparing", True
    Set mFMNodes = New Collection: Set mFMEdges = New Collection: Set mFMIssues = New Collection
    Set mFMSheets = New Collection
    Set mFMNodeIndex = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): mFMNodeIndex.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    Set mFMEdgeIndex = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): mFMEdgeIndex.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    mFMFormulaCount = 0: mFMTotalFormulas = 0: mFMLimited = False
    fmProgressSheet = 0
    For Each ws In mFMSource.Worksheets
        mFMProgressSheetDone = fmProgressSheet
        FM_ShowProgress "1/5 Finding formulas", fmProgressSheet, mFMSource.Worksheets.Count, ws.Name, True
        mFMSheets.Add ws.Name
        Set formulas = FM_CollectFormulaCells(ws)
        If Not formulas Is Nothing Then
            For Each cell In formulas
                If FM_IsFormulaAnchor(cell) Then mFMTotalFormulas = mFMTotalFormulas + 1
            Next cell
        End If
        fmProgressSheet = fmProgressSheet + 1
        FM_ShowProgress "1/5 Finding formulas", fmProgressSheet, mFMSource.Worksheets.Count, _
            "Formulas found: " & Format$(mFMTotalFormulas, "#,##0"), True
    Next ws
    mFMProgressStage = 2
    FM_ShowProgress "2/5 Analyzing references", 0, _
        Application.Min(mFMTotalFormulas, FM_MAX_FORMULAS), "", True
    For Each ws In mFMSource.Worksheets
        Set formulas = FM_CollectFormulaCells(ws)
        If Not formulas Is Nothing Then
            For Each cell In formulas
                If FM_IsFormulaAnchor(cell) Then
                If mFMFormulaCount >= FM_MAX_FORMULAS Then
                    mFMLimited = True
                    Exit For
                End If
                mFMFormulaCount = mFMFormulaCount + 1
                target = FM_EnsureNode(mFMSource.Name, ws.Name, cell.Address(False, False), "formula")
                Set problems = New Collection
                Set refs = FM_ParseFormula(cell, problems)
                For Each ref In refs
                    source = FM_EnsureNode(CStr(ref("book")), CStr(ref("sheet")), CStr(ref("address")), CStr(ref("kind")))
                    FM_AddEdge source, target, CStr(ref("kind")), CStr(ref("note"))
                Next ref
                For Each item In problems
                    FM_AddIssue ws.Name, cell.Address(False, False), FM_GetFormula(cell), CStr(item)
                Next item
                If mFMFormulaCount Mod 25 = 0 Then
                    FM_ShowProgress "2/5 Analyzing references", mFMFormulaCount, _
                        Application.Min(mFMTotalFormulas, FM_MAX_FORMULAS), ws.Name
                End If
                End If
            Next cell
        End If
        If mFMLimited Then Exit For
    Next ws
    If mFMLimited Then FM_AddIssue "", "", "", "Partial analysis: formula scan limit reached."
    FM_ShowProgress "2/5 Analyzing references", mFMFormulaCount, _
        Application.Min(mFMTotalFormulas, FM_MAX_FORMULAS), "", True
    FM_ShowProgress "3/5 Linking ranges", 0, 0, "Preparing", True
    FM_LinkRangeMembers
    mFMProgressStage = 4
    FM_ShowProgress "4/5 Reading sheets", 0, mFMSource.Worksheets.Count, "", True
    mFMWorksheetJSON = FM_SnapshotJSON()
    base = outputFolder & Application.PathSeparator & "FormulaMap_" & FM_SafeName(mFMSource.Name) & "_" & Format$(Now, "yyyymmdd_hhnnss")
    base = FM_UniqueBase(base)
    htmlPath = base & ".html"
    If createExcelReport Then
        Set report = FM_CreateReport()
        FM_AddNativeSpatialMap report
        report.SaveAs Filename:=base & ".xlsx", FileFormat:=xlOpenXMLWorkbook
    End If
    mFMProgressStage = 5
    FM_ShowProgress "5/5 Creating HTML", 0, 0, "Preparing map data", True
    fmProgressData = FM_DataJSON()
    FM_ShowProgress "5/5 Creating HTML", 0, 0, "Building HTML", True
    fmProgressHTML = Replace$(FM_Template(), "__FORMULA_MAP_DATA__", fmProgressData)
    FM_ShowProgress "5/5 Creating HTML", 0, 0, "Saving the file", True
    FM_WriteUTF8 htmlPath, fmProgressHTML
    FM_ShowProgress "5/5 Creating HTML", 1, 1, "Saved", True
    If Not report Is Nothing Then report.Worksheets(1).Activate
    FM_BuildFile = htmlPath
Clean:
    On Error Resume Next
    Application.ScreenUpdating = oldScreen: Application.EnableEvents = oldEvents
    Application.DisplayAlerts = oldAlerts: Application.EnableCancelKey = oldCancel
    Application.StatusBar = oldStatus
    Application.DisplayStatusBar = oldDisplayStatusBar
    mFMProgressBusy = False: mFMProgressStage = 0
    On Error GoTo 0
    If errorNumber <> 0 Then
        If Not report Is Nothing Then
            On Error Resume Next
            report.Close False
            On Error GoTo 0
        End If
        Err.Raise errorNumber, "FormulaMap", errorText
    End If
    If Not silent Then
        MsgBox "Formula map created." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
            "Formulas analyzed: " & Format$(mFMFormulaCount, "#,##0") & vbCrLf & _
            "Review notices: " & mFMIssues.Count & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
            htmlPath & vbCrLf & base & ".xlsx" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
            "Open the HTML to search, zoom, and switch between English and Korean.", vbInformation
    End If
    Exit Function
Failed:
    errorNumber = Err.Number: errorText = Err.Description
    Resume Clean
End Function

Private Function FM_CollectFormulaCells(ByVal ws As Worksheet) As Collection
    Dim result As Collection, scope As Range, hit As Range, nextHit As Range
    Dim firstAddress As String, fmProgressHits As Long
    Set result = New Collection
    Set scope = ws.Cells
    Set hit = scope.Find(What:="=*", After:=ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, ws.Columns.Count), _
        LookIn:=xlFormulas, LookAt:=xlWhole, SearchOrder:=xlByRows, _
        SearchDirection:=xlNext, MatchCase:=False, SearchFormat:=False)
    If Not hit Is Nothing Then
        firstAddress = hit.Address
        Do
            If hit.HasFormula Then result.Add hit
            fmProgressHits = fmProgressHits + 1
            If fmProgressHits Mod 200 = 0 Then
                If mFMProgressStage = 1 Then
                    FM_ShowProgress "1/5 Finding formulas", mFMProgressSheetDone, _
                        mFMSource.Worksheets.Count, ws.Name & " · Found: " & Format$(result.Count, "#,##0")
                ElseIf mFMProgressStage = 2 Then
                    FM_ShowProgress "2/5 Analyzing references", mFMFormulaCount, _
                        Application.Min(mFMTotalFormulas, FM_MAX_FORMULAS), ws.Name & " · Reading formula list"
                End If
            End If
            Set nextHit = scope.Find(What:="=*", After:=hit, _
                LookIn:=xlFormulas, LookAt:=xlWhole, SearchOrder:=xlByRows, _
                SearchDirection:=xlNext, MatchCase:=False, SearchFormat:=False)
            If nextHit Is Nothing Then Exit Do
            Set hit = nextHit
            If hit.Address = firstAddress Then Exit Do
        Loop
    End If
    Set FM_CollectFormulaCells = result
End Function

Private Function FM_IsFormulaAnchor(ByVal cell As Range) As Boolean
    If cell.MergeCells Then
        If cell.Address <> cell.MergeArea.Cells(1, 1).Address Then Exit Function
    End If
    FM_IsFormulaAnchor = CBool(cell.HasFormula)
End Function

Private Function FM_GetFormula(ByVal cell As Range) As String
    On Error Resume Next
    FM_GetFormula = CStr(CallByName(cell, "Formula2", VbGet))
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then Err.Clear: FM_GetFormula = CStr(cell.Formula)
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function FM_EnsureNode(ByVal book As String, ByVal sheet As String, ByVal address As String, ByVal kind As String) As String
    Dim key As String, d As Object, r As Range, ws As Worksheet, cell As Range, displayRange As Range
    If Len(book) = 0 Then book = mFMSource.Name
    key = book & Chr$(30) & sheet & Chr$(30) & Replace$(address, "$", "")
    If mFMNodeIndex.Exists(key) Then
        FM_EnsureNode = mFMNodeIndex(key)
        Exit Function
    End If
    Set d = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    d("id") = "n" & (mFMNodes.Count + 1): d("book") = book
    d("sheet") = sheet: d("address") = Replace$(address, "$", "")
    d("label") = "": d("value") = "": d("formula") = "": d("kind") = kind
    If StrComp(book, mFMSource.Name, vbTextCompare) = 0 And kind <> "external" Then
        On Error Resume Next
        Set ws = mFMSource.Worksheets(sheet)
        If Not ws Is Nothing Then Set r = ws.Range(address)
        On Error GoTo 0
        If Not r Is Nothing Then
            If r.CountLarge = 1 Then
                If r.HasFormula Then d("kind") = "formula": d("formula") = FM_GetFormula(r)
                d("value") = FM_CellText(r)
                d("label") = FM_NearbyLabel(r)
            Else
                d("value") = Format$(r.CountLarge, "#,##0") & " cells"
                d("kind") = "range"
            End If
            d("r1") = r.Row: d("c1") = r.Column
            d("r2") = r.Row + r.Rows.Count - 1: d("c2") = r.Column + r.Columns.Count - 1
            Set displayRange = r
            If r.CountLarge = 1 Then
                If r.MergeCells Then Set displayRange = r.MergeArea
            End If
            d("x") = CDbl(displayRange.Left) * 4 / 3: d("y") = CDbl(displayRange.Top) * 4 / 3
            d("w") = CDbl(displayRange.Width) * 4 / 3: d("h") = CDbl(displayRange.Height) * 4 / 3
        End If
    Else
        d("kind") = "external"
        d("label") = "External workbook"
    End If
    mFMNodes.Add d: mFMNodeIndex.Add key, d("id")
    FM_EnsureNode = d("id")
End Function

Private Function FM_CellText(ByVal cell As Range) As String
    On Error Resume Next
    If IsError(cell.Value2) Then
        FM_CellText = CStr(cell.Text)
    Else
        FM_CellText = CStr(cell.Value2)
    End If
    FM_CellText = Left$(FM_CellText, 400)
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function FM_NearbyLabel(ByVal cell As Range) As String
    Dim neighbor As Range, s As String
    On Error Resume Next
    If cell.Column > 1 Then Set neighbor = cell.Offset(0, -1)
    If Not neighbor Is Nothing Then
        If Not neighbor.HasFormula And VarType(neighbor.Value2) = vbString Then
            s = Trim$(CStr(neighbor.Value2))
            If Len(s) > 0 And Len(s) <= 60 Then FM_NearbyLabel = neighbor.Address(False, False) & ": " & s: Exit Function
        End If
    End If
    Set neighbor = Nothing
    If cell.Row > 1 Then Set neighbor = cell.Offset(-1, 0)
    If Not neighbor Is Nothing Then
        If Not neighbor.HasFormula And VarType(neighbor.Value2) = vbString Then
            s = Trim$(CStr(neighbor.Value2))
            If Len(s) > 0 And Len(s) <= 60 Then FM_NearbyLabel = neighbor.Address(False, False) & ": " & s
        End If
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Sub FM_AddEdge(ByVal source As String, ByVal target As String, ByVal kind As String, ByVal note As String)
    Dim key As String, d As Object
    key = source & ">" & target & ":" & kind
    If mFMEdgeIndex.Exists(key) Then Exit Sub
    Set d = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    d("s") = source: d("t") = target: d("kind") = kind: d("note") = note
    mFMEdges.Add d: mFMEdgeIndex.Add key, True
End Sub

Private Sub FM_AddIssue(ByVal sheet As String, ByVal address As String, ByVal formula As String, ByVal reason As String)
    Dim d As Object
    Set d = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    d("sheet") = sheet: d("address") = address: d("formula") = formula: d("reason") = reason
    mFMIssues.Add d
End Sub

Private Sub FM_LinkRangeMembers()
    Dim ranges As Collection, formulas As Collection, d As Object, f As Object
    Dim r As Range, c As Range, checks As Long, wb As String
    Dim fmProgressRangesDone As Long, fmProgressNodesDone As Long
    Set ranges = New Collection: Set formulas = New Collection
    For Each d In mFMNodes
        fmProgressNodesDone = fmProgressNodesDone + 1
        If fmProgressNodesDone Mod 250 = 0 Then FM_ShowProgress _
            "3/5 Linking ranges", 0, 0, "Classifying: " & Format$(fmProgressNodesDone, "#,##0")
        If d("kind") = "range" And d("book") = mFMSource.Name Then ranges.Add d
        If d("kind") = "formula" Then formulas.Add d
    Next d
    FM_ShowProgress "3/5 Linking ranges", 0, ranges.Count, "Ranges", True
    For Each d In ranges
        Set r = Nothing
        On Error Resume Next
        Set r = mFMSource.Worksheets(d("sheet")).Range(d("address"))
        On Error GoTo 0
        If Not r Is Nothing Then
            For Each f In formulas
                checks = checks + 1
                If checks Mod 2500 = 0 Then FM_ShowProgress "3/5 Linking ranges", _
                    fmProgressRangesDone, ranges.Count, "Ranges completed · Checks: " & Format$(checks, "#,##0")
                If checks > FM_MAX_MEMBERSHIP_CHECKS Then
                    FM_AddIssue "", "", "", "Range membership checks capped; direct reference list retained."
                    FM_ShowProgress "3/5 Linking ranges", fmProgressRangesDone, ranges.Count, _
                        "Check limit reached: " & Format$(FM_MAX_MEMBERSHIP_CHECKS, "#,##0"), True
                    Exit Sub
                End If
                If f("sheet") = d("sheet") And f("book") = d("book") Then
                    Set c = Nothing
                    On Error Resume Next
                    Set c = Application.Intersect(r, mFMSource.Worksheets(f("sheet")).Range(f("address")))
                    On Error GoTo 0
                    If Not c Is Nothing Then FM_AddEdge f("id"), d("id"), "membership", "Formula cell contained in the referenced range"
                End If
            Next f
        End If
        fmProgressRangesDone = fmProgressRangesDone + 1
        FM_ShowProgress "3/5 Linking ranges", fmProgressRangesDone, ranges.Count, _
            "Ranges completed · Checks: " & Format$(checks, "#,##0")
    Next d
    FM_ShowProgress "3/5 Linking ranges", fmProgressRangesDone, ranges.Count, _
        "Completed · Checks: " & Format$(checks, "#,##0"), True
End Sub

Private Function FM_CreateReport() As Workbook
    Dim result As Workbook, overview As Worksheet, detail As Worksheet, refs As Worksheet, issues As Worksheet
    Dim arr() As Variant, i As Long, e As Object, a As Object, b As Object, d As Object
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set result = Workbooks.Add(xlWBATWorksheet)
    Set overview = result.Worksheets(1): overview.Name = "Worksheet relationships"
    Set detail = result.Worksheets.Add(After:=overview): detail.Name = "Cell relationships"
    Set refs = result.Worksheets.Add(After:=detail): refs.Name = "Formula references"
    Set issues = result.Worksheets.Add(After:=refs): issues.Name = "Review notices"
    FM_StyleSheet overview: FM_StyleSheet detail: FM_StyleSheet refs: FM_StyleSheet issues
    overview.Range("B2").Value2 = "Worksheet relationships"
    overview.Range("B4").Value2 = "Workbook: " & mFMSource.Name & "    Formulas analyzed: " & mFMFormulaCount & " / " & Format$(mFMTotalFormulas, "#,##0")
    overview.Range("B5").Value2 = "Source → dependent"
    overview.Range("B6").Value2 = "Connections and boxes are editable. Use the generated HTML for search and full exploration."
    If mFMLimited Then overview.Range("B7").Value2 = "Partial analysis: the 50,000-formula limit was reached."
    FM_DrawSheetMap overview
    detail.Range("B2").Value2 = "Cell relationships"
    detail.Range("B4").Value2 = "Solid: direct reference / Dashed: formula cell contained in a referenced range"
    detail.Range("B5").Value2 = "Up to " & FM_MAX_NATIVE_NODES & " cells/ranges are shown. See Formula references and the HTML for all references."
    detail.Range("B6").Value2 = "Each box shows Sheet!Address, nearby labels, and its formula. Labels are copied with their original addresses."
    FM_DrawCellMap detail
    refs.Range("B2").Value2 = "Formula references"
    refs.Range("B4").Value2 = "Captured: " & Format$(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss") & "    Source: " & mFMSource.Name
    refs.Range("A6:K6").Value2 = Array("Source workbook", "Source worksheet", "Source cell/range", "Dependent worksheet", "Dependent cell", "Relationship type", "Relationship description", "English description", "Dependent formula", "Source labels (original nearby text)", "Note")
    If mFMEdges.Count > 0 Then
        ReDim arr(1 To mFMEdges.Count, 1 To 11)
        i = 0
        For Each e In mFMEdges
            i = i + 1: Set a = mFMNodes(CLng(Mid$(e("s"), 2))): Set b = mFMNodes(CLng(Mid$(e("t"), 2)))
            arr(i, 1) = a("book"): arr(i, 2) = a("sheet"): arr(i, 3) = a("address")
            arr(i, 4) = b("sheet"): arr(i, 5) = b("address"): arr(i, 6) = e("kind")
            If e("kind") = "membership" Then
                arr(i, 7) = FM_NodeText(a) & " is contained in range " & FM_NodeText(b) & "."
                arr(i, 8) = FM_NodeText(a) & " is contained in range " & FM_NodeText(b) & "."
            Else
                arr(i, 7) = FM_NodeText(b) & " references " & FM_NodeText(a) & "."
                arr(i, 8) = FM_NodeText(b) & " references " & FM_NodeText(a) & "."
            End If
            arr(i, 9) = b("formula"): arr(i, 10) = a("label"): arr(i, 11) = e("note")
        Next e
        refs.Range("A7").Resize(mFMEdges.Count, 11).NumberFormat = "@"
        refs.Range("A7").Resize(mFMEdges.Count, 11).Value2 = arr
    End If
    refs.Range("A6:K" & CStr(Application.Max(7, mFMEdges.Count + 6))).AutoFilter
    refs.Columns("A:F").ColumnWidth = 21: refs.Columns("G:I").ColumnWidth = 58: refs.Columns("J:K").ColumnWidth = 38
    refs.Rows(6).RowHeight = 32
    refs.Activate: refs.Range("D7").Select: ActiveWindow.FreezePanes = True
    issues.Range("B2").Value2 = "References to review"
    issues.Range("B4").Value2 = "This is a static analysis of formula references. Conditional formulas include references from every possible branch."
    issues.Range("B5").Value2 = "Review notices for references that depend on calculation, defined names, or table state. Hidden worksheets are also analyzed."
    issues.Range("A7:D7").Value2 = Array("Worksheet", " cells", "Formula", "Reason for review")
    If mFMIssues.Count > 0 Then
        ReDim arr(1 To mFMIssues.Count, 1 To 4): i = 0
        For Each d In mFMIssues
            i = i + 1: arr(i, 1) = d("sheet"): arr(i, 2) = d("address"): arr(i, 3) = d("formula"): arr(i, 4) = d("reason")
        Next d
        issues.Range("A8").Resize(mFMIssues.Count, 4).NumberFormat = "@"
        issues.Range("A8").Resize(mFMIssues.Count, 4).Value2 = arr
    Else
        issues.Range("A8").Value2 = "No specific review notices were detected. Formula calculation results were not validated."
    End If
    issues.Columns("A:B").ColumnWidth = 22: issues.Columns("C").ColumnWidth = 65: issues.Columns("D").ColumnWidth = 110
    issues.Range("A7:D" & CStr(Application.Max(8, mFMIssues.Count + 7))).AutoFilter
    FM_Header refs.Range("A6:K6"): FM_Header issues.Range("A7:D7")
    overview.Activate: ActiveWindow.DisplayGridlines = False: ActiveWindow.Zoom = 85
    Set FM_CreateReport = result
    Exit Function
Failed:
    Dim savedError As Long, savedDescription As String
    savedError = Err.Number: savedDescription = Err.Description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not result Is Nothing Then result.Close False
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise savedError, "FM_CreateReport", savedDescription
End Function

Private Sub FM_StyleSheet(ByVal ws As Worksheet)
    With ws.Range("A1:Q50")
        .Font.Name = "Arial": .Font.Size = 10: .Font.Color = RGB(16, 38, 60)
        .RowHeight = 22
    End With
    ws.Columns("A").ColumnWidth = 3: ws.Columns("B:Q").ColumnWidth = 13
    ws.Range("B2").Font.Size = 16: ws.Range("B2").Font.Bold = True
    ws.Activate: ActiveWindow.DisplayGridlines = False
End Sub

Private Sub FM_Header(ByVal r As Range)
    r.Interior.Color = RGB(16, 38, 60): r.Font.Color = vbWhite: r.Font.Bold = True
    r.HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter: r.VerticalAlignment = xlCenter
End Sub

Private Function FM_NodeText(ByVal d As Object) As String
    If d("book") <> mFMSource.Name Then FM_NodeText = "[" & d("book") & "]"
    FM_NodeText = FM_NodeText & "'" & Replace$(d("sheet"), "'", "''") & "'!" & d("address")
End Function

Private Function FM_Box(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByVal text As String, ByVal x As Single, ByVal y As Single, ByVal width As Single, ByVal height As Single, ByVal accent As Long) As Shape
    Dim s As Shape
    Set s = ws.Shapes.AddShape(5, x, y, width, height)
    s.Fill.ForeColor.RGB = RGB(245, 248, 250): s.Line.ForeColor.RGB = accent
    s.Line.Weight = 1.5
    With s.TextFrame2
        .TextRange.Text = text: .TextRange.Font.Name = "Arial": .TextRange.Font.Size = 10
        .TextRange.Font.Fill.ForeColor.RGB = RGB(16, 38, 60)
        .MarginLeft = 10: .MarginRight = 8: .MarginTop = 8: .MarginBottom = 6
        .WordWrap = -1
    End With
    Set FM_Box = s
End Function

Private Sub FM_Connect(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByVal a As Shape, ByVal b As Shape, ByVal dashed As Boolean)
    Dim line As Shape, sourceSite As Long, targetSite As Long
    If a.Left < b.Left Then
        sourceSite = 4: targetSite = 2
    ElseIf a.Left > b.Left Then
        sourceSite = 2: targetSite = 4
    ElseIf a.Top < b.Top Then
        sourceSite = 3: targetSite = 1
    Else
        sourceSite = 1: targetSite = 3
    End If
    Set line = ws.Shapes.AddConnector(2, 0, 0, 10, 10)
    line.ConnectorFormat.BeginConnect a, sourceSite: line.ConnectorFormat.EndConnect b, targetSite
    line.Line.ForeColor.RGB = RGB(0, 127, 140): line.Line.EndArrowheadStyle = 3
    line.Line.Weight = 1.2
    If dashed Then line.Line.DashStyle = 4
    line.ZOrder 1
End Sub

Private Sub FM_DrawSheetMap(ByVal ws As Worksheet)
    Dim groups As Object, shapes As Object, counts As Object, e As Object, a As Object, b As Object
    Dim key As Variant, parts As Variant, n As Long, label As String, s As Shape
    Set groups = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): Set shapes = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): Set counts = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For Each key In mFMSheets: groups.Add mFMSource.Name & Chr$(30) & CStr(key), CStr(key): Next key
    For Each e In mFMEdges
        If e("kind") <> "membership" Then
            Set a = mFMNodes(CLng(Mid$(e("s"), 2))): Set b = mFMNodes(CLng(Mid$(e("t"), 2)))
            key = a("book") & Chr$(30) & a("sheet")
            If Not groups.Exists(key) Then groups.Add key, "[" & a("book") & "] " & a("sheet")
            If a("book") <> b("book") Or a("sheet") <> b("sheet") Then
                key = a("book") & Chr$(30) & a("sheet") & Chr$(29) & b("book") & Chr$(30) & b("sheet")
                If Not counts.Exists(key) Then counts.Add key, 0
                counts(key) = counts(key) + 1
            End If
        End If
    Next e
    n = 0
    For Each key In groups.Keys
        n = n + 1
        If n > 60 Then Exit For
        Set s = FM_Box(ws, groups(key), 30 + ((n - 1) Mod 3) * 310, 180 + ((n - 1) \ 3) * 160, 230, 70, RGB(0, 127, 140))
        shapes.Add key, s
    Next key
    For Each key In counts.Keys
        parts = Split(key, Chr$(29))
        If shapes.Exists(parts(0)) And shapes.Exists(parts(1)) Then FM_Connect ws, shapes(parts(0)), shapes(parts(1)), False
    Next key
    If groups.Count > 60 Then ws.Range("B8").Value2 = "Up to 60 worksheets are shown. See the HTML for the complete list."
    ws.Range("S2:U2").Value2 = Array("Source", "Dependent", "Direct references")
    ws.Columns("S:T").NumberFormat = "@"
    n = 2
    For Each key In counts.Keys
        n = n + 1: parts = Split(key, Chr$(29))
        ws.Cells(n, 19).Value2 = groups(parts(0)): ws.Cells(n, 20).Value2 = groups(parts(1)): ws.Cells(n, 21).Value2 = counts(key)
    Next key
    ws.Columns("S:T").ColumnWidth = 28: ws.Columns("U").ColumnWidth = 18
    FM_Header ws.Range("S2:U2")
End Sub

Private Sub FM_DrawCellMap(ByVal ws As Worksheet)
    Dim groups As Object, shapes As Object, rows As Object, d As Object, e As Object, s As Shape
    Dim key As String, n As Long, col As Long, r As Long, t As String, hidden As Long
    Set groups = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): Set shapes = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): Set rows = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For Each d In mFMNodes
        n = n + 1
        If n > FM_MAX_NATIVE_NODES Then Exit For
        key = d("book") & Chr$(30) & d("sheet")
        If Not groups.Exists(key) Then groups.Add key, groups.Count: rows.Add key, 0
        col = groups(key): r = rows(key): rows(key) = r + 1
        t = FM_NodeText(d)
        If Len(d("label")) > 0 Then t = t & vbCrLf & d("label")
        If Len(d("formula")) > 0 Then t = t & vbCrLf & Left$(d("formula"), 110)
        If d("kind") = "range" Then t = t & vbCrLf & "Referenced range"
        Set s = FM_Box(ws, t, 30 + col * 290, 170 + r * 120, 250, 96, FM_SheetColor(col))
        shapes.Add d("id"), s
    Next d
    n = 0: hidden = 0
    For Each e In mFMEdges
        If shapes.Exists(e("s")) And shapes.Exists(e("t")) And n < 200 Then
            FM_Connect ws, shapes(e("s")), shapes(e("t")), e("kind") = "membership": n = n + 1
        Else
            hidden = hidden + 1
        End If
    Next e
    ws.Range("B7").Value2 = "Shown: " & shapes.Count & " / " & mFMNodes.Count & " boxes, " & n & " / " & mFMEdges.Count & " connections. All collected data remains in Formula references and the HTML."
End Sub

Private Function FM_SheetColor(ByVal n As Long) As Long
    Select Case n Mod 5
        Case 0: FM_SheetColor = RGB(0, 127, 140)
        Case 1: FM_SheetColor = RGB(40, 93, 158)
        Case 2: FM_SheetColor = RGB(121, 77, 152)
        Case 3: FM_SheetColor = RGB(166, 99, 25)
        Case Else: FM_SheetColor = RGB(47, 126, 80)
    End Select
End Function

Private Function FM_DataJSON() As String
    Dim nodeItems() As String, edgeItems() As String, issueItems() As String, sheetItems() As String
    Dim i As Long, d As Object, nodeText As String, edgeText As String, issueText As String, sheetText As String
    If mFMNodes.Count > 0 Then
        ReDim nodeItems(0 To mFMNodes.Count - 1): i = 0
        For Each d In mFMNodes
            nodeItems(i) = FM_ObjectJSON(d, Array("id", "book", "sheet", "address", "label", "value", "formula", "kind"))
            If d.Exists("r1") Then nodeItems(i) = Left$(nodeItems(i), Len(nodeItems(i)) - 1) & "," & FM_GeometryJSON(d) & "}"
            i = i + 1
            If i Mod 100 = 0 Then FM_ShowProgress "5/5 Preparing cells", _
                i, mFMNodes.Count, "", False
        Next d
        nodeText = Join(nodeItems, ",")
    End If
    If mFMEdges.Count > 0 Then
        ReDim edgeItems(0 To mFMEdges.Count - 1): i = 0
        For Each d In mFMEdges
            edgeItems(i) = FM_ObjectJSON(d, Array("s", "t", "kind", "note")): i = i + 1
            If i Mod 100 = 0 Then FM_ShowProgress "5/5 HTML Preparing references", i, mFMEdges.Count, "", False
        Next d
        edgeText = Join(edgeItems, ",")
    End If
    If mFMIssues.Count > 0 Then
        ReDim issueItems(0 To mFMIssues.Count - 1): i = 0
        For Each d In mFMIssues
            issueItems(i) = FM_ObjectJSON(d, Array("sheet", "address", "formula", "reason")): i = i + 1
            If i Mod 100 = 0 Then FM_ShowProgress "5/5 HTML Preparing notices", i, mFMIssues.Count, "", False
        Next d
        issueText = Join(issueItems, ",")
    End If
    If mFMSheets.Count > 0 Then
        ReDim sheetItems(0 To mFMSheets.Count - 1)
        For i = 1 To mFMSheets.Count: sheetItems(i - 1) = FM_JSON(CStr(mFMSheets(i))): Next i
        sheetText = Join(sheetItems, ",")
    End If
    FM_DataJSON = "{""sourceName"":" & FM_JSON(mFMSource.Name) & ",""analyzedAt"":" & FM_JSON(Format$(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss")) & _
        ",""formulaCount"":" & mFMFormulaCount & ",""totalFormulaCount"":" & Format$(mFMTotalFormulas, "0") & _
        ",""scanLimited"":" & LCase$(CStr(mFMLimited)) & ",""nodes"": [" & nodeText & "],""edges"": [" & edgeText & _
        "],""issues"": [" & issueText & "],""sheets"": [" & sheetText & "],""worksheets"":" & mFMWorksheetJSON & "}"
End Function

Private Function FM_ObjectJSON(ByVal d As Object, ByVal keys As Variant) As String
    Dim parts() As String, i As Long
    ReDim parts(LBound(keys) To UBound(keys))
    For i = LBound(keys) To UBound(keys): parts(i) = FM_JSON(CStr(keys(i))) & ":" & FM_JSON(CStr(d(keys(i)))): Next i
    FM_ObjectJSON = "{" & Join(parts, ",") & "}"
End Function

Private Function FM_JSON(ByVal s As String) As String
    Dim i As Long, n As Long, c As String, items() As String
    If Len(s) = 0 Then FM_JSON = """""": Exit Function
    ReDim items(1 To Len(s))
    For i = 1 To Len(s)
        c = Mid$(s, i, 1): n = AscW(c)
        Select Case n
            Case 34: items(i) = "\"""
            Case 92: items(i) = "\\"
            Case 0 To 31, 60, 62, 38, &H2028, &H2029
                items(i) = "\u" & Right$("0000" & Hex$(n), 4)
            Case Else: items(i) = c
        End Select
    Next i
    FM_JSON = """" & Join(items, "") & """"
End Function

Private Sub FM_WriteUTF8(ByVal path As String, ByVal text As String)
    Dim stream As Object
    Set stream = CreateObject("ADODB.Stream")
    stream.Type = 2: stream.Charset = "utf-8": stream.Open
    stream.WriteText text: stream.SaveToFile path, 1: stream.Close
End Sub

Private Function FM_Base64ToText(ByVal encoded As String) As String
    Dim doc As Object, element As Object, stream As Object
    Set doc = CreateObject("MSXML2.DOMDocument.6.0")
    Set element = doc.createElement("base64")
    element.DataType = "bin.base64": element.Text = encoded
    Set stream = CreateObject("ADODB.Stream")
    stream.Type = 1: stream.Open: stream.Write element.nodeTypedValue
    stream.Position = 0: stream.Type = 2: stream.Charset = "utf-8"
    FM_Base64ToText = stream.ReadText: stream.Close
End Function

Private Function FM_SafeName(ByVal name As String) As String
    Dim c As Variant
    For Each c In Array("\", "/", ":", "*", "?", """", "<", ">", "|")
        name = Replace$(name, CStr(c), "_")
    Next c
    FM_SafeName = Left$(name, 80)
End Function

Private Function FM_UniqueBase(ByVal base As String) As String
    Dim suffix As Long, candidate As String
    candidate = base
    Do While Len(Dir$(candidate & ".xlsx")) > 0 Or Len(Dir$(candidate & ".html")) > 0
        suffix = suffix + 1: candidate = base & "_" & suffix
    Loop
    FM_UniqueBase = candidate
End Function


' Static, read-only A1 formula reference parser. No Evaluate/Calculate/Select.
' Public contract: Collection of dictionaries with book/sheet/address/kind/note.
' Address means a syntactically referenced source, not an evaluated dependency.

Public Function FM_ParseFormula(ByVal formulaCell As Range, ByVal issues As Collection) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, seen As Object, warned As Object
    Dim f As String, p As Long, q As Long, tokenEnd As Long, nextP As Long
    Dim qualifier As String, token As String, addr As String, refKind As String
    Dim structText As String, cellFormula As Variant, scopedFormula As Boolean
    Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set warned = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    seen.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    warned.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    On Error GoTo ParseFailure
    If formulaCell Is Nothing Then GoTo Done
    If formulaCell.Cells.CountLarge <> 1 Then
        FMP_Issue issues, warned, "Parser requires one formula cell."
        GoTo Done
    End If
    If Not formulaCell.HasFormula Then GoTo Done
    On Error Resume Next
    cellFormula = CallByName(formulaCell, "Formula2", VbGet)
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then
        Err.Clear
        cellFormula = formulaCell.Formula
    End If
    On Error GoTo ParseFailure
    f = CStr(cellFormula)
    scopedFormula = FMP_HasScopedFunction(f)
    If scopedFormula Then FMP_Issue issues, warned, "LET/LAMBDA may shadow defined names; unqualified defined names are not resolved in this formula."
    p = 1
    Do While p <= Len(f)
        If Mid$(f, p, 1) = """" Then
            p = FMP_SkipString(f, p)
        ElseIf Mid$(f, p, 1) = "@" Then
            FMP_Issue issues, warned, "Implicit intersection (@): source references are shown; the effective intersected cell is not evaluated."
            p = p + 1
        ElseIf FMP_TryQualifier(f, p, qualifier, q) Then
            If FMP_ReadQualifiedA1(f, q, qualifier, addr, refKind, nextP) Then
                FMP_AddQualified result, seen, issues, warned, formulaCell, qualifier, addr, refKind
                If refKind = "spill" Then FMP_Issue issues, warned, "Spill reference (#): only its anchor/range is recorded; the spill extent is not evaluated."
                FMP_CheckIntersection f, nextP, issues, warned
                p = nextP
            Else
                token = FMP_ReadIdentifier(f, q, tokenEnd)
                If Len(token) > 0 Then
                    If FMP_ReadStructured(f, tokenEnd, structText, nextP) Then
                        FMP_Issue issues, warned, "Sheet-qualified structured reference could not be resolved safely: " & qualifier & "!" & token & structText
                        p = nextP
                    Else
                        FMP_AddQualifiedName result, seen, issues, warned, formulaCell, qualifier, token
                        p = tokenEnd
                    End If
                Else
                    FMP_Issue issues, warned, "Unresolved qualified reference near: " & Mid$(f, p, 70)
                    p = q
                End If
            End If
        ElseIf FMP_IsTokenStart(Mid$(f, p, 1)) And FMP_IsBoundaryBefore(f, p) Then
            token = FMP_ReadIdentifier(f, p, tokenEnd)
            q = FMP_SkipSpace(f, tokenEnd)
            If Len(token) > 0 And Mid$(f, q, 1) = "(" Then
                FMP_CheckFunction token, issues, warned
                p = tokenEnd
            ElseIf Len(token) > 0 And FMP_ReadStructured(f, tokenEnd, structText, nextP) Then
                FMP_AddStructured result, seen, issues, warned, formulaCell, token, structText
                FMP_CheckIntersection f, nextP, issues, warned
                p = nextP
            ElseIf FMP_ReadA1(f, p, addr, refKind, nextP) Then
                FMP_AddSource result, seen, formulaCell.Parent.Parent.Name, formulaCell.Parent.Name, addr, refKind, ""
                If refKind = "spill" Then FMP_Issue issues, warned, "Spill reference (#): only its anchor/range is recorded; the spill extent is not evaluated."
                FMP_CheckIntersection f, nextP, issues, warned
                p = nextP
            ElseIf Len(token) > 0 Then
                If Not scopedFormula Then FMP_AddName result, seen, issues, warned, formulaCell, formulaCell.Parent, token
                p = tokenEnd
            Else
                p = p + 1
            End If
        ElseIf Mid$(f, p, 1) = "[" Then
            If FMP_ReadStructured(f, p, structText, nextP) Then
                FMP_AddStructured result, seen, issues, warned, formulaCell, "", structText
                FMP_CheckIntersection f, nextP, issues, warned
                p = nextP
            Else
                p = p + 1
            End If
        Else
            p = p + 1
        End If
    Loop
Done:
    Set FM_ParseFormula = result
    Exit Function
ParseFailure:
    FMP_Issue issues, warned, "Formula parsing stopped after an error: " & Err.Description
    Resume Done
End Function

Private Sub FMP_CheckIntersection(ByVal f As String, ByVal p As Long, ByVal issues As Collection, ByVal warned As Object)
    Dim q As Long, ch As String
    q = FMP_SkipSpace(f, p)
    If q <= p Then Exit Sub
    ch = Mid$(f, q, 1)
    If FMP_IsTokenStart(ch) Or ch = "'" Or ch = "[" Or ch = "(" Then
        FMP_Issue issues, warned, "Possible reference intersection (space operator): operand ranges are shown; the intersected result is not evaluated."
    End If
End Sub

Private Function FMP_SkipString(ByVal f As String, ByVal p As Long) As Long
    p = p + 1
    Do While p <= Len(f)
        If Mid$(f, p, 1) = """" Then
            If Mid$(f, p + 1, 1) = """" Then
                p = p + 2
            Else
                FMP_SkipString = p + 1
                Exit Function
            End If
        Else
            p = p + 1
        End If
    Loop
    FMP_SkipString = p
End Function

Private Function FMP_SkipSpace(ByVal f As String, ByVal p As Long) As Long
    Do While p <= Len(f)
        If InStr(1, " " & vbTab & vbCr & vbLf, Mid$(f, p, 1), vbBinaryCompare) = 0 Then Exit Do
        p = p + 1
    Loop
    FMP_SkipSpace = p
End Function

Private Function FMP_IsTokenStart(ByVal ch As String) As Boolean
    If Len(ch) = 0 Then Exit Function
    FMP_IsTokenStart = FMP_IsIdentifierChar(ch) Or ch = "$"
End Function

Private Function FMP_IsIdentifierChar(ByVal ch As String) As Boolean
    Dim n As Long
    If Len(ch) = 0 Then Exit Function
    n = AscW(ch)
    FMP_IsIdentifierChar = (ch Like "[A-Za-z0-9_]") Or ch = "." Or ch = "\" Or n < 0 Or n > 127
End Function

Private Function FMP_IsBoundaryBefore(ByVal f As String, ByVal p As Long) As Boolean
    If p <= 1 Then
        FMP_IsBoundaryBefore = True
    Else
        FMP_IsBoundaryBefore = Not (FMP_IsIdentifierChar(Mid$(f, p - 1, 1)) Or Mid$(f, p - 1, 1) = "$")
    End If
End Function

Private Function FMP_ReadIdentifier(ByVal f As String, ByVal p As Long, ByRef endP As Long) As String
    Dim startP As Long
    startP = p
    Do While p <= Len(f)
        If Not FMP_IsIdentifierChar(Mid$(f, p, 1)) Then Exit Do
        p = p + 1
    Loop
    endP = p
    FMP_ReadIdentifier = Mid$(f, startP, p - startP)
End Function

Private Function FMP_TryQualifier(ByVal f As String, ByVal p As Long, ByRef qualifier As String, ByRef afterBang As Long) As Boolean
    Dim q As Long, ch As String, s As String, depth As Long
    If Not FMP_IsBoundaryBefore(f, p) Then Exit Function
    q = p
    If Mid$(f, q, 1) = "'" Then
        q = q + 1
        Do While q <= Len(f)
            ch = Mid$(f, q, 1)
            If ch = "'" Then
                If Mid$(f, q + 1, 1) = "'" Then
                    s = s & "'"
                    q = q + 2
                Else
                    If Mid$(f, q + 1, 1) = "!" Then
                        qualifier = s
                        afterBang = q + 2
                        FMP_TryQualifier = True
                    End If
                    Exit Function
                End If
            Else
                s = s & ch
                q = q + 1
            End If
        Loop
        Exit Function
    End If
    Do While q <= Len(f)
        ch = Mid$(f, q, 1)
        If ch = "!" And depth = 0 Then
            If q > p Then
                qualifier = Mid$(f, p, q - p)
                afterBang = q + 1
                FMP_TryQualifier = True
            End If
            Exit Function
        ElseIf ch = "[" Then
            depth = depth + 1
        ElseIf ch = "]" And depth > 0 Then
            depth = depth - 1
        ElseIf depth = 0 Then
            If Not (FMP_IsIdentifierChar(ch) Or ch = ":") Then Exit Function
        End If
        q = q + 1
    Loop
End Function

Private Function FMP_ReadA1(ByVal f As String, ByVal p As Long, ByRef address As String, ByRef kind As String, ByRef endP As Long) As Boolean
    Dim firstPart As String, secondPart As String, firstKind As String, secondKind As String
    Dim q As Long, r As Long, checkP As Long, ch As String
    If Not FMP_ReadA1Part(f, p, firstPart, firstKind, q) Then Exit Function
    address = firstPart
    kind = "cell"
    checkP = FMP_SkipSpace(f, q)
    If Mid$(f, checkP, 1) = ":" Then
        If Not FMP_ReadA1Part(f, FMP_SkipSpace(f, checkP + 1), secondPart, secondKind, r) Then Exit Function
        If firstKind <> secondKind Then Exit Function
        address = firstPart & ":" & secondPart
        kind = "range"
        q = r
    ElseIf firstKind <> "cell" Then
        Exit Function
    End If
    ch = Mid$(f, q, 1)
    If FMP_IsIdentifierChar(ch) Or ch = "$" Or ch = "!" Or ch = "[" Then Exit Function
    checkP = FMP_SkipSpace(f, q)
    If Mid$(f, checkP, 1) = "(" Then Exit Function
    If ch = "#" Then
        kind = "spill"
        q = q + 1
    End If
    endP = q
    FMP_ReadA1 = True
End Function

Private Function FMP_ReadQualifiedA1(ByVal f As String, ByVal p As Long, ByVal qualifier As String, ByRef address As String, ByRef kind As String, ByRef endP As Long) As Boolean
    Dim firstPart As String, firstKind As String, secondPart As String, secondKind As String
    Dim q As Long, r As Long, s As Long, qualifier2 As String
    If FMP_ReadA1(f, p, address, kind, endP) Then
        FMP_ReadQualifiedA1 = True
        Exit Function
    End If
    If Not FMP_ReadA1Part(f, p, firstPart, firstKind, q) Then Exit Function
    q = FMP_SkipSpace(f, q)
    If Mid$(f, q, 1) <> ":" Then Exit Function
    q = FMP_SkipSpace(f, q + 1)
    If Not FMP_TryQualifier(f, q, qualifier2, r) Then Exit Function
    If StrComp(qualifier, qualifier2, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Exit Function
    If Not FMP_ReadA1Part(f, r, secondPart, secondKind, s) Then Exit Function
    If firstKind <> secondKind Then Exit Function
    If FMP_IsIdentifierChar(Mid$(f, s, 1)) Or Mid$(f, s, 1) = "$" Then Exit Function
    address = firstPart & ":" & secondPart
    kind = "range"
    endP = s
    FMP_ReadQualifiedA1 = True
End Function

Private Function FMP_ReadA1Part(ByVal f As String, ByVal p As Long, ByRef part As String, ByRef kind As String, ByRef endP As Long) As Boolean
    Dim q As Long, startP As Long, col As String, rowText As String, colNum As Long, rowNum As Double, ch As String
    q = p
    If Mid$(f, q, 1) = "$" Then q = q + 1
    startP = q
    Do While q <= Len(f)
        ch = UCase$(Mid$(f, q, 1))
        If ch < "A" Or ch > "Z" Then Exit Do
        q = q + 1
    Loop
    col = UCase$(Mid$(f, startP, q - startP))
    If Len(col) > 3 Then Exit Function
    If Len(col) > 0 Then
        colNum = FMP_ColumnNumber(col)
        If colNum < 1 Or colNum > 16384 Then Exit Function
        If Mid$(f, q, 1) = "$" Then q = q + 1
    End If
    startP = q
    Do While Mid$(f, q, 1) Like "[0-9]" And q <= Len(f)
        q = q + 1
    Loop
    rowText = Mid$(f, startP, q - startP)
    If Len(rowText) > 0 Then
        If Len(rowText) > 7 Then Exit Function
        If Left$(rowText, 1) = "0" Then Exit Function
        rowNum = CDbl(rowText)
        If rowNum < 1 Or rowNum > 1048576 Then Exit Function
    End If
    If Len(col) > 0 And Len(rowText) > 0 Then
        kind = "cell": part = col & CStr(CLng(rowNum))
    ElseIf Len(col) > 0 Then
        kind = "column": part = col
    ElseIf Len(rowText) > 0 Then
        kind = "row": part = CStr(CLng(rowNum))
    Else
        Exit Function
    End If
    endP = q
    FMP_ReadA1Part = True
End Function

Private Function FMP_ColumnNumber(ByVal col As String) As Long
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 1 To Len(col)
        FMP_ColumnNumber = FMP_ColumnNumber * 26 + Asc(Mid$(col, i, 1)) - 64
    Next i
End Function

Private Sub FMP_CheckFunction(ByVal token As String, ByVal issues As Collection, ByVal warned As Object)
    Dim fn As String, p As Long
    fn = UCase$(token)
    p = InStrRev(fn, ".")
    If p > 0 Then fn = Mid$(fn, p + 1)
    Select Case fn
        Case "INDIRECT", "OFFSET"
            FMP_Issue issues, warned, fn & " uses a computed reference; only explicit references in its arguments are recorded."
        Case "LET", "LAMBDA"
            FMP_Issue issues, warned, fn & " scope/variables are not evaluated; explicit cell/range/table references are recorded."
    End Select
End Sub

Private Function FMP_HasScopedFunction(ByVal f As String) As Boolean
    Dim p As Long, q As Long, n As Long, token As String
    p = 1
    Do While p <= Len(f)
        If Mid$(f, p, 1) = """" Then
            p = FMP_SkipString(f, p)
        ElseIf FMP_IsIdentifierChar(Mid$(f, p, 1)) And FMP_IsBoundaryBefore(f, p) Then
            token = FMP_ReadIdentifier(f, p, q)
            n = InStrRev(token, ".")
            If n > 0 Then token = Mid$(token, n + 1)
            If Mid$(f, FMP_SkipSpace(f, q), 1) = "(" Then
                If UCase$(token) = "LET" Or UCase$(token) = "LAMBDA" Then
                    FMP_HasScopedFunction = True
                    Exit Function
                End If
            End If
            p = q
        Else
            p = p + 1
        End If
    Loop
End Function

Private Sub FMP_AddQualified(ByVal result As Collection, ByVal seen As Object, ByVal issues As Collection, ByVal warned As Object, ByVal source As Range, ByVal qualifier As String, ByVal address As String, ByVal kind As String)
    Dim bookName As String, sheetText As String, pathText As String, isExternal As Boolean
    Dim wb As Workbook, ws As Worksheet, firstIndex As Long, lastIndex As Long, i As Long, p As Long
    Set wb = source.Parent.Parent
    FMP_SplitQualifier qualifier, bookName, sheetText, pathText, isExternal
    If isExternal Then
        If StrComp(bookName, wb.Name, vbTextCompare) = 0 And Len(pathText) = 0 Then
            isExternal = False
        Else
            If InStr(sheetText, ":") > 0 Or Len(sheetText) = 0 Then
                FMP_Issue issues, warned, "External reference is not resolved: " & qualifier & "!" & address
            Else
                FMP_AddSource result, seen, pathText & bookName, sheetText, address, "external", "External workbook; existence/value is not verified. " & pathText
                FMP_Issue issues, warned, "External workbook reference is recorded as written; source existence is not verified: " & bookName
            End If
            Exit Sub
        End If
    End If
    p = InStr(sheetText, ":")
    If p > 0 Then
        firstIndex = FMP_SheetIndex(wb, Left$(sheetText, p - 1))
        lastIndex = FMP_SheetIndex(wb, Mid$(sheetText, p + 1))
        If firstIndex = 0 Or lastIndex = 0 Then
            FMP_Issue issues, warned, "3D reference has an unresolved sheet: " & qualifier & "!" & address
            Exit Sub
        End If
        If firstIndex > lastIndex Then
            i = firstIndex: firstIndex = lastIndex: lastIndex = i
        End If
        For i = firstIndex To lastIndex
            If TypeName(wb.Sheets(i)) = "Worksheet" Then
                FMP_AddSource result, seen, wb.Name, wb.Sheets(i).Name, address, "3d", "3D source: " & qualifier & "!" & address
            Else
                FMP_Issue issues, warned, "Non-worksheet in 3D sheet interval was skipped: " & wb.Sheets(i).Name
            End If
        Next i
    Else
        Set ws = FMP_FindSheet(wb, sheetText)
        If ws Is Nothing Then
            FMP_Issue issues, warned, "Referenced worksheet was not found: " & sheetText
        Else
            FMP_AddSource result, seen, wb.Name, ws.Name, address, kind, ""
        End If
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub FMP_SplitQualifier(ByVal qualifier As String, ByRef bookName As String, ByRef sheetText As String, ByRef pathText As String, ByRef external As Boolean)
    Dim leftP As Long, rightP As Long
    bookName = "": pathText = "": sheetText = qualifier: external = False
    leftP = InStrRev(qualifier, "[")
    rightP = InStrRev(qualifier, "]")
    If leftP > 0 And rightP > leftP Then
        bookName = Mid$(qualifier, leftP + 1, rightP - leftP - 1)
        pathText = Left$(qualifier, leftP - 1)
        sheetText = Mid$(qualifier, rightP + 1)
        external = True
    End If
End Sub

Private Function FMP_FindSheet(ByVal wb As Workbook, ByVal name As String) As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set FMP_FindSheet = wb.Worksheets(name)
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function FMP_SheetIndex(ByVal wb As Workbook, ByVal name As String) As Long
    Dim sh As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    Set sh = wb.Sheets(name)
    If Not sh Is Nothing Then FMP_SheetIndex = sh.Index
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Sub FMP_AddQualifiedName(ByVal result As Collection, ByVal seen As Object, ByVal issues As Collection, ByVal warned As Object, ByVal source As Range, ByVal qualifier As String, ByVal token As String)
    Dim bookName As String, sheetText As String, pathText As String, external As Boolean, ws As Worksheet
    FMP_SplitQualifier qualifier, bookName, sheetText, pathText, external
    If external Or InStr(sheetText, ":") > 0 Then
        FMP_Issue issues, warned, "External/3D defined name is not resolved: " & qualifier & "!" & token
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Set ws = FMP_FindSheet(source.Parent.Parent, sheetText)
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        FMP_Issue issues, warned, "Defined-name worksheet was not found: " & qualifier
    Else
        FMP_AddName result, seen, issues, warned, source, ws, token
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub FMP_AddName(ByVal result As Collection, ByVal seen As Object, ByVal issues As Collection, ByVal warned As Object, ByVal source As Range, ByVal scope As Worksheet, ByVal token As String)
    Dim nm As Name, candidate As Name, r As Range, n As String, p As Long, rf As String
    If Len(token) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    If IsNumeric(token) Then Exit Sub
    Select Case UCase$(token)
        Case "TRUE", "FALSE", "_XLFN", "_XLWS": Exit Sub
    End Select
    On Error Resume Next
    For Each candidate In scope.Names
        n = candidate.Name
        p = InStrRev(n, "!")
        If p > 0 Then n = Mid$(n, p + 1)
        If StrComp(n, token, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            Set nm = candidate
            Exit For
        End If
    Next candidate
    If nm Is Nothing Then
        For Each candidate In source.Parent.Parent.Names
            n = candidate.Name
            If InStr(n, "!") = 0 And StrComp(n, token, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
                Set nm = candidate
                Exit For
            End If
        Next candidate
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
    If nm Is Nothing Then
        If Not FMP_FindTable(source.Parent.Parent, token) Is Nothing Then
            FMP_AddStructured result, seen, issues, warned, source, token, "[#Data]"
        End If
        Exit Sub ' Unknown tokens may be LET/LAMBDA variables, not source cells.
    End If
    On Error Resume Next
    rf = nm.RefersTo
    On Error GoTo 0
    If FMP_IsConstantName(rf) Then Exit Sub
    If FMP_NameIsRelative(nm) Then
        FMP_Issue issues, warned, "Relative defined name is not resolved against an active cell: " & nm.Name
        Exit Sub
    End If
    On Error Resume Next
    Set r = nm.RefersToRange
    On Error GoTo 0
    If r Is Nothing Then
        If Not FMP_IsConstantName(rf) Then FMP_Issue issues, warned, "Defined name has no static Range (expression, dynamic or unavailable source): " & nm.Name
    Else
        FMP_AddRange result, seen, r, "name", "Defined name: " & nm.Name
    End If
End Sub

Private Function FMP_NameIsRelative(ByVal nm As Name) As Boolean
    Dim rx As Object, matches As Object, m As Object, s As String
    On Error GoTo Conservative
    s = FMP_StripQualifierAndStrings(nm.RefersToR1C1)
    Set rx = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    rx.Global = True: rx.IgnoreCase = True
    rx.Pattern = "(^|[^A-Za-z0-9_])R(\[[-+]?\d+\]|\d*)C(\[[-+]?\d+\]|\d*)"
    Set matches = rx.Execute(s)
    For Each m In matches
        If InStr(m.Value, "[") > 0 Or Len(m.SubMatches(1)) = 0 Or Len(m.SubMatches(2)) = 0 Then
            FMP_NameIsRelative = True
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next m
    rx.Pattern = "(^|[^A-Za-z0-9_])([RC])(\[[-+]?\d+\]|\d*)(?=$|[^A-Za-z0-9_])"
    Set matches = rx.Execute(s)
    For Each m In matches
        If InStr(m.Value, "[") > 0 Or Len(m.SubMatches(2)) = 0 Then
            FMP_NameIsRelative = True
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next m
    Exit Function
Conservative:
    FMP_NameIsRelative = True
End Function

Private Function FMP_StripQualifierAndStrings(ByVal f As String) As String
    Dim p As Long, q As Long, qualifier As String
    p = 1
    Do While p <= Len(f)
        If Mid$(f, p, 1) = """" Then
            q = FMP_SkipString(f, p)
            FMP_StripQualifierAndStrings = FMP_StripQualifierAndStrings & Space$(q - p)
            p = q
        ElseIf FMP_TryQualifier(f, p, qualifier, q) Then
            FMP_StripQualifierAndStrings = FMP_StripQualifierAndStrings & Space$(q - p)
            p = q
        Else
            FMP_StripQualifierAndStrings = FMP_StripQualifierAndStrings & Mid$(f, p, 1)
            p = p + 1
        End If
    Loop
End Function

Private Function FMP_IsConstantName(ByVal f As String) As Boolean
    Dim s As String
    s = Trim$(f)
    If Left$(s, 1) = "=" Then s = Mid$(s, 2)
    If IsNumeric(s) Then FMP_IsConstantName = True: Exit Function
    If UCase$(s) = "TRUE" Or UCase$(s) = "FALSE" Then FMP_IsConstantName = True: Exit Function
    If Left$(s, 1) = """" And FMP_SkipString(s, 1) > Len(s) Then FMP_IsConstantName = True
End Function

Private Function FMP_ReadStructured(ByVal f As String, ByVal p As Long, ByRef text As String, ByRef endP As Long) As Boolean
    Dim q As Long, depth As Long, ch As String
    If Mid$(f, p, 1) <> "[" Then Exit Function
    q = p
    Do While q <= Len(f)
        ch = Mid$(f, q, 1)
        If ch = "'" And InStr(1, "[]#'@", Mid$(f, q + 1, 1), vbBinaryCompare) > 0 And q < Len(f) Then
            q = q + 2
        Else
            If ch = "[" Then depth = depth + 1
            If ch = "]" Then depth = depth - 1
            q = q + 1
            If depth = 0 Then
                text = Mid$(f, p, q - p)
                endP = q
                FMP_ReadStructured = True
                Exit Function
            End If
        End If
    Loop
End Function

Private Sub FMP_AddStructured(ByVal result As Collection, ByVal seen As Object, ByVal issues As Collection, ByVal warned As Object, ByVal source As Range, ByVal tableName As String, ByVal spec As String)
    Dim lo As ListObject, candidate As ListObject, ws As Worksheet, leaves As Collection
    Dim rowRange As Range, selected As Range, part As Range, colRange As Range, r As Range
    Dim cols As New Collection, item As Variant, raw As String, text As String, thisRow As Boolean
    Dim hasAll As Boolean, hasHeaders As Boolean, hasData As Boolean, hasTotals As Boolean
    Dim hadItem As Boolean, i As Long, minCol As Long, maxCol As Long, colIndex As Long
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If Len(tableName) > 0 Then
        Set lo = FMP_FindTable(source.Parent.Parent, tableName)
    Else
        For Each candidate In source.Parent.ListObjects
            If Not Application.Intersect(source, candidate.Range) Is Nothing Then
                Set lo = candidate
                Exit For
            End If
        Next candidate
    End If
    If lo Is Nothing Then
        FMP_Issue issues, warned, "Structured reference has no resolvable table: " & tableName & spec
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Set leaves = FMP_StructuredLeaves(spec)
    If leaves.Count = 0 Then GoTo Failed
    thisRow = FMP_StructuredHasThisRow(spec)
    hadItem = thisRow
    For Each item In leaves
        raw = CStr(item)
        text = FMP_UnescapeStructured(raw)
        If Left$(raw, 1) = "#" Then
            hadItem = True
            Select Case UCase$(Trim$(text))
                Case "#ALL": hasAll = True
                Case "#HEADERS": hasHeaders = True
                Case "#DATA": hasData = True
                Case "#TOTALS": hasTotals = True
                Case "#THIS ROW": thisRow = True
                Case Else: GoTo Failed
            End Select
        ElseIf Left$(raw, 1) = "@" Then
            thisRow = True
            hadItem = True
            text = FMP_UnescapeStructured(Mid$(raw, 2))
            If Len(text) > 0 Then cols.Add text
        Else
            cols.Add text
        End If
    Next item
    If thisRow Then
        If hasAll Or hasHeaders Or hasTotals Then GoTo Failed
        If lo.DataBodyRange Is Nothing Then GoTo Failed
        Set rowRange = Application.Intersect(lo.DataBodyRange, source.EntireRow)
        If rowRange Is Nothing Then GoTo Failed
    ElseIf hasAll Then
        Set rowRange = lo.Range
    Else
        If Not hadItem Then hasData = True
        If hasData And Not lo.DataBodyRange Is Nothing Then Set rowRange = lo.DataBodyRange
        If hasHeaders Then
            Set part = lo.HeaderRowRange
            FMP_UnionInto rowRange, part
        End If
        If hasTotals Then
            Set part = Nothing
            On Error Resume Next
            Set part = lo.TotalsRowRange
            On Error GoTo Failed
            If part Is Nothing Then
                FMP_Issue issues, warned, "Table totals reference has no visible totals row: " & lo.Name & spec
            Else
                FMP_UnionInto rowRange, part
            End If
        End If
    End If
    If rowRange Is Nothing Then
        FMP_Issue issues, warned, "Structured reference has no cells in the current table state: " & lo.Name & spec
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If cols.Count = 0 Then
        Set selected = rowRange
    ElseIf cols.Count = 2 And InStr(spec, "]:[") > 0 Then
        minCol = FMP_ListColumnIndex(lo, CStr(cols(1)))
        maxCol = FMP_ListColumnIndex(lo, CStr(cols(2)))
        If minCol = 0 Or maxCol = 0 Then GoTo Failed
        If minCol > maxCol Then i = minCol: minCol = maxCol: maxCol = i
        Set colRange = lo.Range.Columns(minCol).Resize(lo.Range.Rows.Count, maxCol - minCol + 1)
        Set selected = Application.Intersect(rowRange, colRange)
    Else
        For Each item In cols
            colIndex = FMP_ListColumnIndex(lo, CStr(item))
            If colIndex = 0 Then GoTo Failed
            Set part = Application.Intersect(rowRange, lo.ListColumns(colIndex).Range)
            FMP_UnionInto selected, part
        Next item
    End If
    If selected Is Nothing Then GoTo Failed
    FMP_AddRange result, seen, selected, "table", "Table reference: " & lo.Name & spec
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    FMP_Issue issues, warned, "Structured reference was not resolved safely: " & tableName & spec
End Sub

Private Function FMP_FindTable(ByVal wb As Workbook, ByVal tableName As String) As ListObject
    Dim ws As Worksheet, lo As ListObject
    For Each ws In wb.Worksheets
        For Each lo In ws.ListObjects
            If StrComp(lo.Name, tableName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Or StrComp(lo.DisplayName, tableName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
                Set FMP_FindTable = lo
                Exit Function
            End If
        Next lo
    Next ws
End Function

Private Function FMP_StructuredHasThisRow(ByVal spec As String) As Boolean
    Dim p As Long
    p = 1
    Do While p <= Len(spec)
        If Mid$(spec, p, 1) = "'" And p < Len(spec) And InStr(1, "[]#'@", Mid$(spec, p + 1, 1), vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then
            p = p + 2
        Else
            If Mid$(spec, p, 2) = "[@" Then
                FMP_StructuredHasThisRow = True
                Exit Function
            End If
            p = p + 1
        End If
    Loop
End Function

Private Function FMP_StructuredLeaves(ByVal spec As String) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, p As Long, ch As String, depth As Long, s As String, hadChild As Boolean
    Dim stackChild As Object
    Set stackChild = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    p = 1
    Do While p <= Len(spec)
        ch = Mid$(spec, p, 1)
        If ch = "'" And p < Len(spec) And InStr(1, "[]#'@", Mid$(spec, p + 1, 1), vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then
            s = s & ch & Mid$(spec, p + 1, 1)
            p = p + 2
        ElseIf ch = "[" Then
            If depth > 0 Then stackChild(CStr(depth)) = True
            depth = depth + 1
            stackChild(CStr(depth)) = False
            s = ""
            p = p + 1
        ElseIf ch = "]" Then
            If depth > 0 Then
                If Not CBool(stackChild(CStr(depth))) Then result.Add s
                stackChild.Remove CStr(depth)
                depth = depth - 1
                s = ""
            End If
            p = p + 1
        Else
            If depth > 0 Then s = s & ch
            p = p + 1
        End If
    Loop
    Set FMP_StructuredLeaves = result
End Function

Private Function FMP_UnescapeStructured(ByVal s As String) As String
    Dim p As Long, ch As String
    p = 1
    Do While p <= Len(s)
        ch = Mid$(s, p, 1)
        If ch = "'" And p < Len(s) And InStr(1, "[]#'@", Mid$(s, p + 1, 1), vbBinaryCompare) > 0 Then
            p = p + 1
            ch = Mid$(s, p, 1)
        End If
        FMP_UnescapeStructured = FMP_UnescapeStructured & ch
        p = p + 1
    Loop
End Function

Private Function FMP_ListColumnIndex(ByVal lo As ListObject, ByVal columnName As String) As Long
    Dim c As ListColumn
    For Each c In lo.ListColumns
        If StrComp(c.Name, columnName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            FMP_ListColumnIndex = c.Index
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next c
End Function

Private Sub FMP_UnionInto(ByRef target As Range, ByVal part As Range)
    If part Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    If target Is Nothing Then
        Set target = part
    Else
        Set target = Application.Union(target, part)
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub FMP_AddRange(ByVal result As Collection, ByVal seen As Object, ByVal r As Range, ByVal kind As String, ByVal note As String)
    Dim a As Range
    For Each a In r.Areas
        FMP_AddSource result, seen, a.Parent.Parent.Name, a.Parent.Name, a.Address(False, False, xlA1), kind, note
    Next a
End Sub

Private Sub FMP_AddSource(ByVal result As Collection, ByVal seen As Object, ByVal book As String, ByVal sheet As String, ByVal address As String, ByVal kind As String, ByVal note As String)
    Dim key As String, d As Object
    address = FMP_CanonicalAddress(address)
    key = book & Chr$(30) & sheet & Chr$(30) & address
    If seen.Exists(key) Then
        Set d = seen(key)
        If Len(note) > 0 And InStr(1, d("note"), note, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            If Len(d("note")) > 0 Then d("note") = d("note") & "; "
            d("note") = d("note") & note
        End If
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Set d = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    d.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    d.Add "book", book
    d.Add "sheet", sheet
    d.Add "address", address
    d.Add "kind", kind
    d.Add "note", note
    seen.Add key, d
    result.Add d
End Sub

Private Function FMP_CanonicalAddress(ByVal address As String) As String
    Dim pieces As Variant, firstPart As String, secondPart As String, firstKind As String, secondKind As String
    Dim q As Long, c1 As Long, c2 As Long, r1 As Long, r2 As Long, t As Long, p As Long
    address = UCase$(Replace(address, "$", ""))
    FMP_CanonicalAddress = address
    pieces = Split(address, ":")
    If UBound(pieces) <> 1 Then Exit Function
    If Not FMP_ReadA1Part(CStr(pieces(0)), 1, firstPart, firstKind, q) Then Exit Function
    If Not FMP_ReadA1Part(CStr(pieces(1)), 1, secondPart, secondKind, q) Then Exit Function
    If firstKind <> secondKind Then Exit Function
    Select Case firstKind
        Case "column"
            c1 = FMP_ColumnNumber(firstPart): c2 = FMP_ColumnNumber(secondPart)
            If c1 > c2 Then t = c1: c1 = c2: c2 = t
            FMP_CanonicalAddress = FMP_ColumnLabel(c1) & ":" & FMP_ColumnLabel(c2)
        Case "row"
            r1 = CLng(firstPart): r2 = CLng(secondPart)
            If r1 > r2 Then t = r1: r1 = r2: r2 = t
            FMP_CanonicalAddress = CStr(r1) & ":" & CStr(r2)
        Case "cell"
            p = 1
            Do While Mid$(firstPart, p, 1) Like "[A-Z]": p = p + 1: Loop
            c1 = FMP_ColumnNumber(Left$(firstPart, p - 1)): r1 = CLng(Mid$(firstPart, p))
            p = 1
            Do While Mid$(secondPart, p, 1) Like "[A-Z]": p = p + 1: Loop
            c2 = FMP_ColumnNumber(Left$(secondPart, p - 1)): r2 = CLng(Mid$(secondPart, p))
            If c1 > c2 Then t = c1: c1 = c2: c2 = t
            If r1 > r2 Then t = r1: r1 = r2: r2 = t
            FMP_CanonicalAddress = FMP_ColumnLabel(c1) & CStr(r1)
            If c1 <> c2 Or r1 <> r2 Then FMP_CanonicalAddress = FMP_CanonicalAddress & ":" & FMP_ColumnLabel(c2) & CStr(r2)
    End Select
End Function

Private Function FMP_ColumnLabel(ByVal columnNumber As Long) As String
    Do While columnNumber > 0
        columnNumber = columnNumber - 1
        FMP_ColumnLabel = Chr$(65 + columnNumber Mod 26) & FMP_ColumnLabel
        columnNumber = columnNumber \ 26
    Loop
End Function

Private Sub FMP_Issue(ByVal issues As Collection, ByVal warned As Object, ByVal message As String)
    If issues Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    If warned Is Nothing Then
        issues.Add message
    ElseIf Not warned.Exists(message) Then
        warned.Add message, True
        issues.Add message
    End If
End Sub


' Read-only worksheet context and exact Excel point-to-CSS-pixel geometry.
Private Function FM_Num(ByVal value As Double) As String
    FM_Num = Trim$(Str$(value))
End Function

Private Function FM_GeometryJSON(ByVal d As Object) As String
    Dim fields As Variant, pieces() As String, i As Long
    fields = Array("r1", "c1", "r2", "c2", "x", "y", "w", "h")
    ReDim pieces(0 To UBound(fields))
    For i = 0 To UBound(fields): pieces(i) = FM_JSON(CStr(fields(i))) & ":" & FM_Num(CDbl(d(fields(i)))): Next i
    FM_GeometryJSON = Join(pieces, ",")
End Function

Private Function FM_SnapshotJSON() As String
    Dim ws As Worksheet, snapshots() As String, i As Long
    ReDim snapshots(0 To mFMSource.Worksheets.Count - 1)
    For Each ws In mFMSource.Worksheets
        mFMProgressSheetDone = i
