        FM_ShowProgress "4/5 Reading sheets", i, mFMSource.Worksheets.Count, ws.Name, True
        snapshots(i) = FM_OneSnapshotJSON(ws): i = i + 1
        FM_ShowProgress "4/5 Reading sheets", i, mFMSource.Worksheets.Count, ws.Name, True
    Next ws
    FM_SnapshotJSON = "[" & Join(snapshots, ",") & "]"
End Function

Private Function FM_OneSnapshotJSON(ByVal ws As Worksheet) As String
    Const MAX_GEOMETRY_ROWS As Long = 2000
    Const MAX_GEOMETRY_COLS As Long = 256
    Const MAX_CONTEXT_CELLS As Long = 20000
    Dim used As Range, rEnd As Long, cEnd As Long, gRows As Long, gCols As Long
    Dim rows() As String, cols() As String, captures As Object, merges As Object
    Dim rr As Long, cc As Long, d As Object, key As Variant, parts As Variant
    Dim cell As Range, merged As Range, texts As Collection, t As String
    Dim r As Long, c As Long, r1 As Long, r2 As Long, c1 As Long, c2 As Long
    Dim cellTexts() As String, mergeTexts() As String, count As Long, i As Long, limited As Boolean
    Dim color As Long, fontColor As Long, bold As Boolean, f As String, value As Variant
    Dim fullWidth As Double, fullHeight As Double, width As Double, height As Double
    Dim fmProgressNodesDone As Long, fmProgressCellsDone As Long
    Set used = ws.UsedRange
    rEnd = used.Row + used.Rows.Count - 1: cEnd = used.Column + used.Columns.Count - 1
    gRows = Application.Min(MAX_GEOMETRY_ROWS, rEnd): gCols = Application.Min(MAX_GEOMETRY_COLS, cEnd)
    fullWidth = (ws.Cells(1, cEnd).Left + ws.Cells(1, cEnd).Width) * 4 / 3
    fullHeight = (ws.Cells(rEnd, 1).Top + ws.Cells(rEnd, 1).Height) * 4 / 3
    width = (ws.Cells(1, gCols).Left + ws.Cells(1, gCols).Width) * 4 / 3
    height = (ws.Cells(gRows, 1).Top + ws.Cells(gRows, 1).Height) * 4 / 3
    ReDim rows(0 To gRows - 1): ReDim cols(0 To gCols - 1)
    For r = 1 To gRows
        rows(r - 1) = "{""index"":" & r & ",""height"":" & FM_Num(ws.Rows(r).Height * 4 / 3) & ",""hidden"":" & LCase$(CStr(ws.Rows(r).Hidden)) & "}"
        If r Mod 100 = 0 Then FM_ShowProgress "4/5 Reading sheets", _
            mFMProgressSheetDone, mFMSource.Worksheets.Count, ws.Name & " · Row geometry: " & r & "/" & gRows
    Next r
    For c = 1 To gCols
        cols(c - 1) = "{""index"":" & c & ",""width"":" & FM_Num(ws.Columns(c).Width * 4 / 3) & ",""hidden"":" & LCase$(CStr(ws.Columns(c).Hidden)) & "}"
        If c Mod 50 = 0 Then FM_ShowProgress "4/5 Reading sheets", _
            mFMProgressSheetDone, mFMSource.Worksheets.Count, ws.Name & " · Column geometry: " & c & "/" & gCols
    Next c
    Set captures = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): Set merges = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For rr = 1 To Application.Min(gRows, 100)
        For cc = 1 To Application.Min(gCols, 40)
            captures(CStr(rr) & ":" & CStr(cc)) = True
        Next cc
    Next rr
    ' Capture context around distant referenced cells without moving them closer.
    For Each d In mFMNodes
        fmProgressNodesDone = fmProgressNodesDone + 1
        If fmProgressNodesDone Mod 250 = 0 Then FM_ShowProgress "4/5 Reading sheets", _
            mFMProgressSheetDone, mFMSource.Worksheets.Count, ws.Name & " · Checking nodes: " & Format$(fmProgressNodesDone, "#,##0")
        If d("book") = mFMSource.Name And d("sheet") = ws.Name And d.Exists("r1") Then
            r1 = Application.Max(1, CLng(d("r1")) - 3): c1 = Application.Max(1, CLng(d("c1")) - 2)
            r2 = Application.Min(gRows, CLng(d("r1")) + 3): c2 = Application.Min(gCols, CLng(d("c1")) + 3)
            For rr = r1 To r2
                For cc = c1 To c2
                    If captures.Count < MAX_CONTEXT_CELLS Then
                        captures(CStr(rr) & ":" & CStr(cc)) = True
                    ElseIf Not captures.Exists(CStr(rr) & ":" & CStr(cc)) Then
                        limited = True
                    End If
                Next cc
            Next rr
        End If
    Next d
    limited = limited Or rEnd > 100 Or cEnd > 40 Or rEnd > gRows Or cEnd > gCols
    Set texts = New Collection
    For Each key In captures.Keys
        parts = Split(CStr(key), ":"): r = CLng(parts(0)): c = CLng(parts(1))
        Set cell = ws.Cells(r, c)
        value = cell.Value2
        If IsError(value) Or cell.HasFormula Or Not IsEmpty(value) Then
            Set merged = cell
            If cell.MergeCells Then
                Set merged = cell.MergeArea
                If Not merges.Exists(merged.Address) Then
                    merges.Add merged.Address, "{""r1"":" & merged.Row & ",""c1"":" & merged.Column & _
                        ",""r2"":" & (merged.Row + merged.Rows.Count - 1) & ",""c2"":" & (merged.Column + merged.Columns.Count - 1) & _
                        ",""x"":" & FM_Num(merged.Left * 4 / 3) & ",""y"":" & FM_Num(merged.Top * 4 / 3) & _
                        ",""w"":" & FM_Num(merged.Width * 4 / 3) & ",""h"":" & FM_Num(merged.Height * 4 / 3) & "}"
                End If
            End If
            color = cell.Interior.Color: fontColor = cell.Font.Color: bold = cell.Font.Bold
            On Error Resume Next
            color = cell.DisplayFormat.Interior.Color
            fontColor = cell.DisplayFormat.Font.Color
            bold = cell.DisplayFormat.Font.Bold
            On Error GoTo 0
            f = "": If cell.HasFormula Then f = FM_GetFormula(cell)
            t = "{""r"":" & r & ",""c"":" & c & ",""address"":" & FM_JSON(cell.Address(False, False)) & _
                ",""text"":" & FM_JSON(CStr(cell.Text)) & ",""formula"":" & FM_JSON(f) & _
                ",""fill"":" & FM_JSON(FM_ColorHex(color)) & ",""fontColor"":" & FM_JSON(FM_ColorHex(fontColor)) & _
                ",""bold"":" & LCase$(CStr(bold)) & ",""format"":" & FM_JSON(CStr(cell.NumberFormat)) & _
                ",""x"":" & FM_Num(merged.Left * 4 / 3) & ",""y"":" & FM_Num(merged.Top * 4 / 3) & _
                ",""w"":" & FM_Num(merged.Width * 4 / 3) & ",""h"":" & FM_Num(merged.Height * 4 / 3) & "}"
            texts.Add t
        End If
        fmProgressCellsDone = fmProgressCellsDone + 1
        If fmProgressCellsDone Mod 100 = 0 Then FM_ShowProgress "4/5 Reading sheets", _
            mFMProgressSheetDone, mFMSource.Worksheets.Count, ws.Name & " · Cells: " & _
            Format$(fmProgressCellsDone, "#,##0") & "/" & Format$(captures.Count, "#,##0")
    Next key
    If texts.Count > 0 Then
        ReDim cellTexts(0 To texts.Count - 1)
        For i = 1 To texts.Count: cellTexts(i - 1) = texts(i): Next i
        t = Join(cellTexts, ",")
    Else
        t = ""
    End If
    Dim mergeJSON As String
    If merges.Count > 0 Then
        ReDim mergeTexts(0 To merges.Count - 1): i = 0
        For Each key In merges.Keys: mergeTexts(i) = merges(key): i = i + 1: Next key
        mergeJSON = Join(mergeTexts, ",")
    End If
    FM_OneSnapshotJSON = "{""name"":" & FM_JSON(ws.Name) & ",""book"":" & FM_JSON(mFMSource.Name) & _
        ",""used"":{""r1"":" & used.Row & ",""c1"":" & used.Column & ",""r2"":" & rEnd & ",""c2"":" & cEnd & "}" & _
        ",""size"":{""width"":" & FM_Num(width) & ",""height"":" & FM_Num(height) & "}" & _
        ",""fullSize"":{""width"":" & FM_Num(fullWidth) & ",""height"":" & FM_Num(fullHeight) & "}" & _
        ",""columns"": [" & Join(cols, ",") & "],""rows"": [" & Join(rows, ",") & "],""merges"": [" & mergeJSON & _
        "],""cells"": [" & t & "],""snapshotLimited"":" & LCase$(CStr(limited)) & ",""hidden"":" & LCase$(CStr(ws.Visible <> xlSheetVisible)) & "}"
End Function

Private Function FM_ColorHex(ByVal color As Long) As String
    If color < 0 Then color = 0
    FM_ColorHex = "#" & Right$("0" & Hex$(color And 255), 2) & _
        Right$("0" & Hex$((color \ 256) And 255), 2) & Right$("0" & Hex$((color \ 65536) And 255), 2)
End Function


' Appended to the FormulaMap module; mFMSource/mFMNodes belong to that module.
' Navigation stays in this Excel instance and never saves/calculates the source.
Private Function FM_NavState() As Object
    Static state As Object
    If state Is Nothing Then Set state = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set FM_NavState = state
End Function

Public Function FM_PrepareNavigationSession(ByVal htmlPath As String) As String
    Dim state As Object, fso As Object, allowed As Object, d As Object, item As Variant
    Dim folder As String, scriptPath As String, json As String, nodes() As String, n As Long
    Dim port As Long, command As String, shell As Object, deadline As Date, url As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    FM_StopNavigation
    If mFMSource Is Nothing Then Err.Raise 5, , "Build the source map first."
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If Not fso.FileExists(htmlPath) Then Err.Raise 53, , "Map HTML was not found."
    folder = Environ$("TEMP") & Application.PathSeparator & "ExcelSpatialMap"
    If Not fso.FolderExists(folder) Then fso.CreateFolder folder
    folder = folder & Application.PathSeparator & fso.GetTempName
    fso.CreateFolder folder
    scriptPath = folder & Application.PathSeparator & "navigation_bridge.ps1"
    FM_WriteUTF8 scriptPath, FM_BridgeScript()
    Set state = FM_NavState(): state.RemoveAll
    Set allowed = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): allowed.CompareMode = vbBinaryCompare
    ReDim nodes(0 To mFMNodes.Count)
    For Each d In mFMNodes
        If StrComp(CStr(d("book")), mFMSource.Name, vbTextCompare) = 0 And d.Exists("r1") Then
            If d("kind") <> "external" Then
                item = CStr(d("sheet")) & Chr$(30) & UCase$(Replace$(CStr(d("address")), "$", ""))
                If Not allowed.Exists(item) Then
                    allowed.Add item, True
                    nodes(n) = "{""sheet"":" & FM_JSON(CStr(d("sheet"))) & ",""address"":" & FM_JSON(CStr(d("address"))) & "}": n = n + 1
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next d
    If n = 0 Then Err.Raise 5, , "No source cells are available for navigation."
    ReDim Preserve nodes(0 To n - 1)
    Randomize: port = 40000 + Int(Rnd() * 20000)
    state.Add "source", mFMSource
    state.Add "fullPath", mFMSource.FullName
    state.Add "allowed", allowed
    state.Add "folder", folder
    state.Add "active", True
    state.Add "expires", DateAdd("h", 2, Now)
    state.Add "scheduled", False
    state.Add "macro", "'" & Replace$(ThisWorkbook.Name, "'", "''") & "'!FM_PollNavigation"
    json = "{""Port"":" & CStr(port) & ",""SessionDirectory"":" & FM_JSON(folder) & _
        ",""HtmlPath"":" & FM_JSON(fso.GetAbsolutePathName(htmlPath)) & ",""SourceName"":" & FM_JSON(mFMSource.Name) & _
        ",""SourceFullPath"":" & FM_JSON(mFMSource.FullName) & ",""LifetimeSeconds"":7200,""Allowed"":" & "[" & Join(nodes, ",") & "]}"
    FM_NavWrite folder & "\config.json", json
    FM_PrepareNavigationSession = folder
    Exit Function
Failed:
    command = Err.Description
    FM_StopNavigation
    Err.Raise 5, "FormulaMap navigation setup", command
End Function

Public Function StartFormulaMapNavigation(ByVal htmlPath As String, Optional ByVal openBrowser As Boolean = True) As String
    Dim state As Object, fso As Object, shell As Object
    Dim folder As String, scriptPath As String, command As String, url As String, deadline As Date
    On Error GoTo Failed
    folder = FM_PrepareNavigationSession(htmlPath)
    Set state = FM_NavState()
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    scriptPath = folder & Application.PathSeparator & "navigation_bridge.ps1"
    FM_PollNavigation
    Set shell = CreateObject("WScript.Shell")
    command = """" & Environ$("SystemRoot") & "\System32\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0\powershell.exe"" -NoLogo -NoProfile -NonInteractive -File """ & scriptPath & """ -ConfigPath """ & folder & "\config.json"""
    shell.Run command, 0, False
    deadline = DateAdd("s", 8, Now)
    Do While Now < deadline
        If fso.FileExists(folder & "\ready.txt") Then
            url = Trim$(FM_NavRead(folder & "\ready.txt"))
            If Left$(url, 17) <> "http://127.0.0.1:" Then Err.Raise 5, , "Invalid navigation address."
            state.Add "url", url
            StartFormulaMapNavigation = url
            If openBrowser Then shell.Run """" & url & """", 1, False
            Exit Function
        End If
        If fso.FileExists(folder & "\startup-error.txt") Then Err.Raise 5, , FM_NavRead(folder & "\startup-error.txt")
        DoEvents
    Loop
    Err.Raise 5, , "Navigation could not start; check PowerShell policy or port availability."
Failed:
    command = Err.Description
    FM_StopNavigation
    Err.Raise 5, "FormulaMap navigation", command
End Function

Public Sub FM_PollNavigation()
    Dim state As Object, fso As Object, folder As String, requestPath As String
    Dim command As String, parts As Variant, id As String, sheet As String, address As String
    Dim reason As String, okay As Boolean, source As Workbook, ws As Worksheet, target As Range
    On Error GoTo PollFailed
    Set state = FM_NavState()
    If Not state.Exists("active") Then Exit Sub
    If Not state("active") Then Exit Sub
    If state("scheduled") Then
        On Error Resume Next
        Application.OnTime EarliestTime:=CDate(state("nextTime")), Procedure:=CStr(state("macro")), Schedule:=False
        Err.Clear
        On Error GoTo PollFailed
    End If
    state("scheduled") = False
    folder = CStr(state("folder"))
    If Now >= CDate(state("expires")) Then FM_StopNavigation: Exit Sub
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If Not fso.FolderExists(folder) Or fso.FileExists(folder & "\stop") Then FM_StopNavigation: Exit Sub
    FM_NavWrite folder & "\heartbeat", Format$(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss")
    reason = FM_NavSourceReason(state)
    FM_NavWrite folder & "\state.json", "{""alive"":" & LCase$(CStr(Len(reason) = 0)) & ",""reason"":" & FM_JSON(reason) & "}"
    requestPath = folder & "\request.txt"
    If fso.FileExists(requestPath) Then
        command = FM_NavRead(requestPath)
        fso.DeleteFile requestPath, True
        parts = Split(command, "|")
        If UBound(parts) = 2 Then
            id = parts(0): sheet = FM_Base64ToText(CStr(parts(1))): address = parts(2)
            If Len(id) = 32 And Len(sheet) <= 31 And Len(address) <= 80 Then
                If Len(reason) = 0 Then
                    If Not state("allowed").Exists(sheet & Chr$(30) & UCase$(Replace$(address, "$", ""))) Then
                        reason = "not_in_exported_map"
                    Else
                        Set source = state("source")
                        Set ws = source.Worksheets(sheet)
                        If ws.Visible <> xlSheetVisible Then
                            reason = "hidden_sheet"
                        Else
                            Set target = ws.Range(address)
                            If StrComp(target.Address(False, False), Replace$(address, "$", ""), vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then
                                reason = "invalid_address"
                            Else
                                Application.Goto Reference:=target, Scroll:=True
                                okay = (ActiveWorkbook Is source)
                                If okay Then okay = (ActiveSheet Is ws)
                                If okay Then okay = (Selection.Address(False, False) = target.Address(False, False))
                                If Not okay Then reason = "selection_not_confirmed"
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
                FM_NavAck folder, id, okay, sheet, address, reason
            End If
        End If
    End If
    If reason = "source_closed" Or reason = "source_path_changed" Then
        FM_StopNavigation
        Exit Sub
    End If
ScheduleNext:
    If state("active") Then
        state("nextTime") = DateAdd("s", 1, Now)
        Application.OnTime EarliestTime:=CDate(state("nextTime")), Procedure:=CStr(state("macro")), Schedule:=True
        state("scheduled") = True
    End If
    Exit Sub
PollFailed:
    On Error Resume Next
    If Len(id) > 0 Then FM_NavAck folder, id, False, sheet, address, "excel_navigation_failed"
    If Not state Is Nothing Then
        If state.Exists("active") Then
            If state("active") Then
                Err.Clear
                state("nextTime") = DateAdd("s", 1, Now)
                Application.OnTime EarliestTime:=CDate(state("nextTime")), Procedure:=CStr(state("macro")), Schedule:=True
                state("scheduled") = True
            End If
        End If
    End If
End Sub

Public Sub FM_StopNavigation()
    Dim state As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    Set state = FM_NavState()
    If state.Exists("active") Then
        state("active") = False
        If state.Exists("scheduled") Then
            If state("scheduled") Then Application.OnTime EarliestTime:=CDate(state("nextTime")), Procedure:=CStr(state("macro")), Schedule:=False
            state("scheduled") = False
        End If
        If state.Exists("folder") Then FM_NavWrite CStr(state("folder")) & "\stop", "closed"
    End If
End Sub

Private Function FM_NavSourceReason(ByVal state As Object) As String
    Dim book As Workbook, source As Workbook, found As Boolean
    On Error GoTo ClosedSource
    Set source = state("source")
    For Each book In Application.Workbooks
        If book Is source Then found = True: Exit For
    Next book
    If Not found Then GoTo ClosedSource
    If StrComp(source.FullName, CStr(state("fullPath")), vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then
        FM_NavSourceReason = "source_path_changed"
    End If
    Exit Function
ClosedSource:
    FM_NavSourceReason = "source_closed"
End Function

Private Sub FM_NavAck(ByVal folder As String, ByVal id As String, ByVal okay As Boolean, ByVal sheet As String, ByVal address As String, ByVal reason As String)
    FM_NavWrite folder & "\ack.json", "{""id"":" & FM_JSON(id) & ",""ok"":" & LCase$(CStr(okay)) & _
        ",""sheet"":" & FM_JSON(sheet) & ",""address"":" & FM_JSON(address) & ",""reason"":" & FM_JSON(reason) & "}"
End Sub

Private Sub FM_NavWrite(ByVal path As String, ByVal value As String)
    Dim stream As Object, fso As Object, temp As String
    Set stream = CreateObject("ADODB.Stream")
    stream.Type = 2: stream.Charset = "utf-8": stream.Open
    stream.WriteText value
    temp = path & ".writing": stream.SaveToFile temp, 2: stream.Close
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If fso.FileExists(path) Then fso.DeleteFile path, True
    fso.MoveFile temp, path
End Sub

Private Function FM_NavRead(ByVal path As String) As String
    Dim stream As Object
    Set stream = CreateObject("ADODB.Stream")
    stream.Type = 2: stream.Charset = "utf-8": stream.Open
    stream.LoadFromFile path: FM_NavRead = stream.ReadText: stream.Close
End Function


' Append to FormulaMap: an offline, native Excel worksheet-position map.
' The source is read only. All shapes and hyperlinks are created in the report.
Private Sub FM_AddNativeSpatialMap(ByVal report As Workbook)
    Const MAX_SHEETS As Long = 20
    Const MAX_CONTEXT_PER_SHEET As Long = 180
    Const MAX_NODES As Long = 700
    Const MAX_EDGES As Long = 120
    Const MAX_ROWS As Long = 2000
    Const MAX_COLS As Long = 256
    Const CARD_W As Double = 620
    Const CARD_H As Double = 390
    Const MAP_W As Double = 580
    Const MAP_H As Double = 322
    Dim map As Worksheet, sourceSheet As Worksheet, used As Range, view As Range
    Dim constants As Range, formulas As Range, context As Range, cell As Range, display As Range
    Dim nodeShapes As Object, transforms As Object, seen As Object, d As Object, e As Object
    Dim node As Shape, connector As Shape, frame As Shape, tr As Variant
    Dim sheetIndex As Long, lastRow As Long, lastCol As Long, shown As Long, n As Long
    Dim omitted As Long, clipped As Long, countContext As Long, contextOmitted As Long
    Dim nodeCount As Long, edgeCount As Long, eligibleEdges As Long
    Dim cardLeft As Double, cardTop As Double, originX As Double, originY As Double
    Dim factor As Double, sourceW As Double, sourceH As Double
    Dim x As Double, y As Double, w As Double, h As Double, r As Long, c As Long
    Dim text As String, note As String, key As String, color As Long
    Dim fontSize As Double, fontColor As Long, isBold As Boolean, fontValue As Variant
    If mFMSource Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    Set map = report.Worksheets.Add(Before:=report.Worksheets(1))
    map.Name = "Cell position map"
    map.Range("A1:AR400").Font.Name = "Arial": map.Range("A1:AR400").Font.Size = 10
    map.Range("B2").Value2 = "Formula relationships in worksheet positions"
    map.Range("B2").Font.Size = 19: map.Range("B2").Font.Bold = True
    map.Range("B4").Value2 = "Blue = formula / Orange = input / Teal = referenced range. Click a cell or range to open its source position."
    map.Range("B5").Value2 = "This miniature map preserves the A1 origin, blank space, row heights, and column widths. Click an item to read its details."
    map.Range("B6").Value2 = "Display limits: 20 worksheets, 180 context cells per sheet, 700 referenced items, and 120 direct connections. Geometry is limited to A1:IV2000; external workbook references are excluded."
    map.Range("B7").Value2 = "Source: " & mFMSource.FullName
    map.Columns("A:A").ColumnWidth = 3
    map.Columns("B:AR").ColumnWidth = 4
    Set nodeShapes = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set transforms = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For Each sourceSheet In mFMSource.Worksheets
        If sheetIndex >= MAX_SHEETS Then Exit For
        sheetIndex = sheetIndex + 1
        cardLeft = 24 + ((sheetIndex - 1) Mod 2) * (CARD_W + 28)
        cardTop = 145 + ((sheetIndex - 1) \ 2) * (CARD_H + 26)
        Set used = sourceSheet.UsedRange
        lastRow = Application.Min(MAX_ROWS, used.Row + used.Rows.Count - 1)
        lastCol = Application.Min(MAX_COLS, used.Column + used.Columns.Count - 1)
        If used.Row + used.Rows.Count - 1 > MAX_ROWS Or used.Column + used.Columns.Count - 1 > MAX_COLS Then clipped = clipped + 1
        Set view = sourceSheet.Range(sourceSheet.Cells(1, 1), sourceSheet.Cells(lastRow, lastCol))
        sourceW = sourceSheet.Cells(1, lastCol).Left + sourceSheet.Cells(1, lastCol).Width
        sourceH = sourceSheet.Cells(lastRow, 1).Top + sourceSheet.Cells(lastRow, 1).Height
        If sourceW <= 0 Then sourceW = 1
        If sourceH <= 0 Then sourceH = 1
        factor = Application.Min(MAP_W / sourceW, MAP_H / sourceH)
        originX = cardLeft + 28: originY = cardTop + 49
        transforms.Add sourceSheet.Name, Array(originX, originY, factor, lastRow, lastCol)
        Set frame = map.Shapes.AddShape(msoShapeRectangle, cardLeft, cardTop, CARD_W, CARD_H)
        frame.Fill.Visible = msoFalse: frame.Line.ForeColor.RGB = RGB(195, 208, 215)
        frame.Line.Weight = 0.8
        Set node = FMNS_Text(map, sourceSheet.Name, cardLeft + 14, cardTop + 8, CARD_W - 28, 20, 13, True, RGB(22, 53, 70))
        FMNS_Link map, node, sourceSheet.Name, "A1", "Open source worksheet"
        note = "A1:" & sourceSheet.Cells(lastRow, lastCol).Address(False, False) & "  / Actual-position scale, " & Format$(factor * 100, "0") & "%"
        If sourceSheet.Visible <> xlSheetVisible Then note = note & " / Hidden worksheet: unhide in Excel before opening"
        If used.Row + used.Rows.Count - 1 > MAX_ROWS Or used.Column + used.Columns.Count - 1 > MAX_COLS Then note = note & "  / Partial range omitted"
        Set node = FMNS_Text(map, note, cardLeft + 14, cardTop + 30, CARD_W - 28, 14, 8, False, RGB(93, 113, 125))
        ' Sparse row/column labels anchor the miniatures to actual Excel coordinates.
        For c = 1 To lastCol
            If c = 1 Or c Mod 4 = 0 Or c = lastCol Then
                x = originX + sourceSheet.Cells(1, c).Left * factor
                If sourceSheet.Cells(1, c).Width > 0 Then Set node = FMNS_Text(map, Split(sourceSheet.Cells(1, c).Address(True, False), "$", 2)(0), x, originY - 13, 24, 12, 6, False, RGB(111, 128, 138))
            End If
        Next c
        For r = 1 To lastRow
            If r = 1 Or r Mod Application.Max(10, CLng(lastRow / 10)) = 0 Or r = lastRow Then
                y = originY + sourceSheet.Cells(r, 1).Top * factor
                If sourceSheet.Cells(r, 1).Height > 0 Then Set node = FMNS_Text(map, CStr(r), cardLeft + 5, y, 21, 10, 6, False, RGB(111, 128, 138))
            End If
        Next r
        ' Enumerate populated cells, rather than scanning a potentially huge empty grid.
        Set constants = Nothing: Set formulas = Nothing: Set context = Nothing
        On Error Resume Next
        Set constants = view.SpecialCells(xlCellTypeConstants)
        Set formulas = view.SpecialCells(xlCellTypeFormulas)
        On Error GoTo 0
        If Not constants Is Nothing Then Set context = Application.Intersect(view, constants)
        If Not formulas Is Nothing Then
            If context Is Nothing Then
                Set context = Application.Intersect(view, formulas)
            Else
                Set context = Application.Union(context, Application.Intersect(view, formulas))
            End If
        End If
        Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): countContext = 0
        If Not context Is Nothing Then
            For Each cell In context.Cells
                Set display = cell
                If cell.MergeCells Then Set display = cell.MergeArea
                key = display.Address
                If Not seen.Exists(key) Then
                    seen.Add key, True
                    If countContext >= MAX_CONTEXT_PER_SHEET Then
                        contextOmitted = contextOmitted + Application.Max(0, context.CountLarge - countContext)
                        Exit For
                    ElseIf display.Left < sourceW And display.Top < sourceH And display.Width > 0 And display.Height > 0 Then
                        countContext = countContext + 1
                        text = Left$(Replace$(Replace$(FM_CellText(display.Cells(1, 1)), vbCr, " "), vbLf, " "), 45)
                        x = originX + display.Left * factor: y = originY + display.Top * factor
                        w = Application.Min(display.Width, sourceW - display.Left) * factor
                        h = Application.Min(display.Height, sourceH - display.Top) * factor
                        If w >= 1 And h >= 1 Then
                            fontSize = 10: fontColor = RGB(45, 63, 74): isBold = False
                            fontValue = cell.Font.Size
                            If Not IsNull(fontValue) Then
                                If IsNumeric(fontValue) Then fontSize = CDbl(fontValue)
                            End If
                            fontValue = cell.Font.Color
                            If Not IsNull(fontValue) Then
                                If IsNumeric(fontValue) Then
                                    If CLng(fontValue) >= 0 Then fontColor = CLng(fontValue)
                                End If
                            End If
                            fontValue = cell.Font.Bold
                            If Not IsNull(fontValue) Then isBold = CBool(fontValue)
                            Set node = FMNS_Text(map, text, x, y, w, h, Application.Min(7, Application.Max(4, fontSize * factor)), isBold, fontColor)
                            color = cell.Interior.Color
                            If color <> RGB(255, 255, 255) Then node.Fill.Visible = msoTrue: node.Fill.ForeColor.RGB = color
                            FMNS_Link map, node, sourceSheet.Name, display.Cells(1, 1).Address(False, False), text
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            Next cell
        End If
        DoEvents
    Next sourceSheet
    ' Range boundaries are transparent; text beneath them remains recognizable.
    For Each d In mFMNodes
        If StrComp(CStr(d("book")), mFMSource.Name, vbTextCompare) = 0 And CStr(d("kind")) <> "external" Then
            If transforms.Exists(CStr(d("sheet"))) Then
                tr = transforms(CStr(d("sheet")))
                Set display = Nothing
                On Error Resume Next
                Set display = mFMSource.Worksheets(CStr(d("sheet"))).Range(CStr(d("address")))
                On Error GoTo 0
                If Not display Is Nothing Then
                    If display.CountLarge = 1 Then
                        If display.MergeCells Then Set display = display.MergeArea
                    End If
                    If display.Row <= CLng(tr(3)) And display.Column <= CLng(tr(4)) And display.Width > 0 And display.Height > 0 And nodeCount < MAX_NODES Then
                        sourceW = mFMSource.Worksheets(CStr(d("sheet"))).Cells(1, CLng(tr(4))).Left + mFMSource.Worksheets(CStr(d("sheet"))).Cells(1, CLng(tr(4))).Width
                        sourceH = mFMSource.Worksheets(CStr(d("sheet"))).Cells(CLng(tr(3)), 1).Top + mFMSource.Worksheets(CStr(d("sheet"))).Cells(CLng(tr(3)), 1).Height
                        x = CDbl(tr(0)) + display.Left * CDbl(tr(2)): y = CDbl(tr(1)) + display.Top * CDbl(tr(2))
                        w = Application.Max(2.5, Application.Min(display.Width, sourceW - display.Left) * CDbl(tr(2)))
                        h = Application.Max(2.5, Application.Min(display.Height, sourceH - display.Top) * CDbl(tr(2)))
                        color = RGB(214, 142, 29)
                        If CStr(d("kind")) = "formula" Then color = RGB(42, 112, 196)
                        If CStr(d("kind")) = "range" Then color = RGB(24, 137, 132)
                        Set node = map.Shapes.AddShape(msoShapeRectangle, x, y, w, h)
                        node.Fill.ForeColor.RGB = color: node.Fill.Transparency = 0.9
                        node.Line.ForeColor.RGB = color: node.Line.Weight = 0.8
                        node.AlternativeText = "'" & CStr(d("sheet")) & "'!" & CStr(d("address")) & " " & CStr(d("label"))
                        FMNS_Link map, node, CStr(d("sheet")), CStr(d("address")), CStr(d("label"))
                        nodeShapes.Add CStr(d("id")), node: nodeCount = nodeCount + 1
                    Else
                        omitted = omitted + 1
                    End If
                End If
            Else
                omitted = omitted + 1
            End If
        End If
    Next d
    ' Light arrows use the true locations; caps keep the static workbook usable.
    For Each e In mFMEdges
        If CStr(e("kind")) <> "membership" Then
            If nodeShapes.Exists(CStr(e("s"))) And nodeShapes.Exists(CStr(e("t"))) Then
                eligibleEdges = eligibleEdges + 1
                If edgeCount < MAX_EDGES Then
                    Set node = nodeShapes(CStr(e("s"))): Set frame = nodeShapes(CStr(e("t")))
                    Set connector = map.Shapes.AddConnector(msoConnectorStraight, node.Left + node.Width / 2, node.Top + node.Height / 2, frame.Left + frame.Width / 2, frame.Top + frame.Height / 2)
                    connector.Line.ForeColor.RGB = RGB(94, 131, 153): connector.Line.Transparency = 0.78
                    connector.Line.Weight = 0.65: connector.Line.EndArrowheadStyle = msoArrowheadTriangle
                    connector.ZOrder msoSendToBack
                    edgeCount = edgeCount + 1
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next e
    map.Range("B9").Value2 = "Shown: " & sheetIndex & " / " & mFMSource.Worksheets.Count & " worksheets, referenced cells/ranges: " & nodeCount & ", direct connections: " & edgeCount & ". Omitted items: " & omitted & ", context cells: " & contextOmitted & " cells, connections: " & (eligibleEdges - edgeCount) & ", worksheets with clipped geometry: " & clipped & "."
    map.PageSetup.Orientation = xlLandscape
    map.PageSetup.Zoom = False: map.PageSetup.FitToPagesWide = 1: map.PageSetup.FitToPagesTall = False
    map.Activate
    On Error Resume Next
    ActiveWindow.DisplayGridlines = False: ActiveWindow.Zoom = 75
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Private Function FMNS_Text(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByVal text As String, ByVal x As Double, ByVal y As Double, ByVal w As Double, ByVal h As Double, ByVal size As Double, ByVal bold As Boolean, ByVal color As Long) As Shape
    Dim shape As Shape
    Set shape = ws.Shapes.AddTextbox(msoTextOrientationHorizontal, x, y, w, h)
    shape.Line.Visible = msoFalse: shape.Fill.Visible = msoFalse
    With shape.TextFrame2
        .MarginLeft = 0: .MarginRight = 0: .MarginTop = 0: .MarginBottom = 0
        .WordWrap = msoFalse
        .TextRange.Text = text
        .TextRange.Font.Name = "Arial"
        .TextRange.Font.Size = size
        .TextRange.Font.Bold = IIf(bold, msoTrue, msoFalse)
        .TextRange.Font.Fill.ForeColor.RGB = color
    End With
    Set FMNS_Text = shape
End Function

Private Sub FMNS_Link(ByVal ws As Worksheet, ByVal shape As Shape, ByVal sheetName As String, ByVal address As String, ByVal label As String)
    Dim target As String, tip As String
    If Len(mFMSource.Path) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    target = "'" & Replace$(sheetName, "'", "''") & "'!" & address
    tip = Left$(target & "  " & label & "  (Open source cell)", 250)
    ws.Hyperlinks.Add Anchor:=shape, Address:=mFMSource.FullName, SubAddress:=target, ScreenTip:=tip
End Sub


' Each percentage is the completed/total count of the displayed stage or
' named subtask, never a guessed elapsed-time percentage or ETA.
Private Sub FM_ShowProgress(ByVal stage As String, ByVal completed As Double, ByVal total As Double, _
    Optional ByVal detail As String = "", Optional ByVal force As Boolean = False)
    Dim tick As Single, elapsed As Single, percent As Long, message As String
    tick = Timer
    elapsed = tick - mFMProgressLastTick
    If elapsed < 0 Then elapsed = elapsed + 86400!
    If Not force And mFMProgressLastTick >= 0 And elapsed < 0.15! Then Exit Sub
    mFMProgressLastTick = tick
    message = stage
    If total > 0 Then
        If completed < 0 Then completed = 0
        If completed > total Then completed = total
        percent = Fix(completed * 100# / total)
        message = message & ": " & Format$(completed, "#,##0") & "/" & Format$(total, "#,##0") & _
            " (" & CStr(percent) & "%)"
    End If
    If Len(detail) > 0 Then message = message & " · " & detail
    Application.StatusBar = message
    DoEvents
End Sub

Private Function FM_Template() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & FM_TemplatePart1()
    s = s & FM_TemplatePart2()
    s = s & FM_TemplatePart3()
    s = s & FM_TemplatePart4()
    s = s & FM_TemplatePart5()
    s = s & FM_TemplatePart6()
    s = s & FM_TemplatePart7()
    s = s & FM_TemplatePart8()
    s = s & FM_TemplatePart9()
    s = s & FM_TemplatePart10()
    s = s & FM_TemplatePart11()
    s = s & FM_TemplatePart12()
    s = s & FM_TemplatePart13()
    s = s & FM_TemplatePart14()
    s = s & FM_TemplatePart15()
    s = s & FM_TemplatePart16()
    FM_Template = FM_Base64ToText(s)
End Function

Private Function FM_BridgeScript() As String
    Dim s As String
    s = s & FM_BridgeScriptPart1()
    s = s & FM_BridgeScriptPart2()
    FM_BridgeScript = FM_Base64ToText(s)
End Function
